Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对四个有害语言检测数据集进行大规模分析，整合了标注者特征、语言属性及其交互作用。研究发现交互效应至关重要，揭示了以往被忽视的交叉影响，词汇线索和标注者态度起关键作用，但效应模式在不同数据集间差异显著，警示了泛化和迁移的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然关注标注者差异（谁在标注）并收集了丰富的社会人口统计信息，但忽视了被标注内容（如语言属性）以及两者交互作用的影响。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对四个有害语言检测参考数据集进行大规模统计分析，整合标注者特征、语言属性及其交互作用，采用统计建模方法揭示各因素及其交互效应。

Result: 交互效应至关重要，揭示了以往被忽视的交叉影响；词汇线索和标注者态度对标注结果有显著作用；但不同数据集间的效应模式差异显著。

Conclusion: 在有害语言检测中，标注者特征与语言属性的交互作用不可忽视，但跨数据集的泛化和迁移需谨慎对待。

Abstract: Human label variation has been established as a central phenomenon in NLP: the perspectives different annotators have on the same item need to be embraced. Data collection practices thus shifted towards increasing the annotator numbers and releasing disaggregated datasets, harmful language being most resourced due to its high subjectivity. While this resulted in rich information about \textit{who} annotated (sociodemographics, attitudes, etc.), the \textit{what} (e.g., linguistic properties of items), and their interplay has received little attention. We present the first large-scale analysis of four reference datasets for harmful language detection, bringing together annotator characteristics, linguistic properties of the items, and their interactions in a statistically informed picture. We find that interactions are crucial, revealing intersectional effects ignored in previous work, and that a strong role is played by lexical cues and annotator attitudes. Effect patterns, however, vary considerably across datasets. This urges caution about generalization and transferability.

</details>


### [38] [Teaching Thinking Models to Reason with Tools: A Full-Pipeline Recipe for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06326)
*Qianjia Cheng,Yuchen Zhang,Zhilin Wang,Yuxin Zuo,Shunkai Zhang,Yuchen Fan,Yu Qiao,Bowen Zhou,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng,Yun Luo,Ganqu Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将工具使用能力注入强推理模型的完整方案（TIR配方），在不牺牲纯文本推理能力的前提下，通过可控的监督微调和稳定的强化学习，在Qwen3 4B和30B模型上实现了AIME 2025上96.7%和99.2%的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具集成推理（TIR）本应增强推理能力，但实际中发现即便强推理模型几乎不调用工具，工具启用评估反而会降低推理性能。因此需要一种方法，在保持模型原有纯文本推理能力的同时，自然注入工具使用行为。

Method: 提出一个四阶段TIR配方：(i) 优先选择适合工具增强的问题生成可学习的教师轨迹进行SFT；(ii) 控制工具使用轨迹比例以防止纯文本推理能力的灾难性遗忘；(iii) 以pass@k和响应长度为优化目标而非训练损失；(iv) 在合适的SFT初始化和显式防模式崩溃机制上，进行基于可验证奖励的稳定强化学习（RLVR）。

Result: 将配方应用于Qwen3 thinking模型（4B和30B），在多个基准测试中达到开源模型SOTA性能：AIME 2025上4B模型96.7%、30B模型99.2%。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的SFT数据选择和比例控制，以及稳定的RL训练，可以在不损失纯文本推理能力的前提下有效注入工具使用能力，该方法在不同规模模型上均有效且表现优异。

Abstract: Tool-integrated reasoning (TIR) offers a direct way to extend thinking models beyond the limits of text-only reasoning. Paradoxically, we observe that tool-enabled evaluation can degrade reasoning performance even when the strong thinking models make almost no actual tool calls. In this paper, we investigate how to inject natural tool-use behavior into a strong thinking model without sacrificing its no-tool reasoning ability, and present a comprehensive TIR recipe. We highlight that (i) the effectiveness of TIR supervised fine-tuning (SFT) hinges on the learnability of teacher trajectories, which should prioritize problems inherently suited for tool-augmented solutions; (ii) controlling the proportion of tool-use trajectories could mitigate the catastrophic forgetting of text-only reasoning capacity; (iii) optimizing for pass@k and response length instead of training loss could maximize TIR SFT gains while preserving headroom for reinforcement learning (RL) exploration; (iv) a stable RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) stage, built upon suitable SFT initialization and explicit safeguards against mode collapse, provides a simple yet remarkably effective solution. When applied to Qwen3 thinking models at 4B and 30B scales, our recipe yields models that achieve state-of-the-art performance in a wide range of benchmarks among open-source models, such as 96.7% and 99.2% on AIME 2025 for 4B and 30B, respectively.

</details>


### [39] [Measuring Evaluation-Context Divergence in Open-Weight LLMs: A Paired-Prompt Protocol with Pilot Evidence of Alignment-Pipeline-Specific Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06327)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"评估-情境差异"概念，指模型在评估框架、部署交互或中性提示下对相同任务表现出不同行为。通过配对提示协议测量开源LLM的该差异，发现不同模型家族存在显著异质性：OLMo-3-Instruct是唯一的"评估谨慎"型模型，而Mistral、Phi、Llama等则是"部署谨慎"型。对齐训练是翻转方向的关键阶段，且该异质性依赖评判模型的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全基准测试常被用作模型部署行为的证据，但若模型行为取决于提示是否看起来像评估，这种推断就不可靠。现有研究未系统量化评估框架与部署情境下行为差异的方向和大小，尤其在不同模型家族间的异质性。

Method: 提出配对提示协议：对同一任务构建三种情境（评估、部署、中性），控制释义变体、基准熟悉度和评判框架敏感性。在5个指令微调检查点（4个开源家族）和1对匹配的OLMo-3基座/指令消融模型上测试（20个配对项目，每个检查点840个生成）。

Result: OLMo-3-Instruct是唯一"评估谨慎"模型：评估框架下拒绝率比中性高11.8pp（p=0.007），有害遵从比部署低3.6pp（p=0.024）。Mistral-Small-3.2、Phi-3.5-mini、Llama-3.1-8B是"部署谨慎"模型，评估vs部署的拒绝效应为-9到-20pp。基座OLMo-3也呈现部署谨慎模式，说明对齐是翻转方向的关键阶段。Llama-3.1-70B方向不变但幅度减弱，排除"小模型效应"。跨家族异质性依赖评判模型，Llama-Guard-3-8B评判保留了OLMo的评估谨慎方向但压平了跨家族对比。

Conclusion: 评估-情境差异在开源LLM中普遍存在但方向高度异质，取决于模型家族和对齐阶段；评判模型的选择显著影响结论；安全基准的生态效度需要审慎对待。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks are routinely treated as evidence about how a language model will behave once deployed, but this inference is fragile if behavior depends on whether a prompt looks like an evaluation. We define evaluation-context divergence as an observable within-item change in behavior induced by framing a fixed task as an evaluation, a live deployment interaction, or a neutral request, and present a paired-prompt protocol that measures it in open-weight LLMs while controlling for paraphrase variation, benchmark familiarity, and judge framing-sensitivity.
  Across five instruction-tuned checkpoints from four open-weight families plus a matched OLMo-3 base/instruct ablation ($20$ paired items, $840$ generations per checkpoint), we find striking heterogeneity. OLMo-3-Instruct alone is eval-cautious -- evaluation framing raises refusal vs. neutral by $11.8$pp ($p=0.007$) and reduces harmful compliance vs. deployment by $3.6$pp ($p=0.024$, $0/20$ items inverted) -- while Mistral-Small-3.2, Phi-3.5-mini, and Llama-3.1-8B are deployment-cautious}, with marginal eval-vs-deployment refusal effects of $-9$ to $-20$pp. The matched OLMo-3 base also exhibits the deployment-cautious pattern, identifying alignment as the inversion stage; within Llama-3.1, the $70$B model preserves direction with attenuated magnitude, ruling out a simple ``small-model effect that reverses at scale.'' One caveat: the cross-family heterogeneity is judge-dependent. Re-judging with a different-family safety classifier (Llama-Guard-3-8B) preserves the within-OLMo eval-cautious direction but flattens the cross-family contrast, indicating that the two judges operationalize distinct constructs.

</details>


### [40] [Don't Lose Focus: Activation Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06342)
*Haoyan Luo,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SKOP方法，通过关键正交投影约束注意力重路由，在保持95%以上引导效果的同时，将效用退化降低5-7倍，尤其是在长上下文检索场景中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导（Activation Steering）能通过干预内部表示控制LLM行为，但常常导致推理和检索性能下降。作者认为主要原因在于注意力重路由——引导向量改变了查询-键匹配，使注意力从上下文重要标记偏移到信息量较少的标记上。

Method: 提出SKOP（Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections），通过关键正交投影约束有害的注意力重路由，保留模型依赖的少数焦点标记上的注意力模式，同时允许在非关键的尾标记间重新分配注意力。

Result: 在多个引导基准上，SKOP实现了最佳联合引导-效用权衡，效用退化降低5-7倍，同时保留超过95%的原始引导效果。在长上下文检索场景中，SKOP通过避免注意力重路由维持了稳健性能。

Conclusion: 注意力重路由是激活引导导致性能下降的主要原因，SKOP通过约束该现象有效缓解了引导与效用之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Activation steering controls LLM behaviour towards target behaviour by intervening in internal representations, yet it often degrades reasoning and retrieval performance. We argue that a primary cause of this trade-off is attention rerouting: steering vectors alter query-key matching, shifting attention away from contextually important tokens toward less informative ones. To address this, we propose Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections (SKOP), a steering method that constrains harmful attention rerouting without eliminating steering efficacy. SKOP achieves this by preserving attention patterns on a small set of focus tokens the model relies on for reasoning and retrieval, while allowing redistribution among less critical tail tokens. Across multiple steering benchmarks, we show that SKOP achieves the best joint steering-utility trade-off, reducing utility degradation by 5-7x while retaining over 95% of vanilla steering efficacy. Our results further suggest that, in long-context retrieval settings where vanilla steering approaches are ineffective, SKOP can maintain robust performance by avoiding attention rerouting.

</details>


### [41] [SEQUOR: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Realistic Constraint Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06353)
*Beatriz Canaverde,Duarte M. Alves,José Pombal,Giuseppe Attanasio,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEQUOR是一个用于评估长多轮对话中指令遵循能力的自动化基准测试。实验发现，即使只遵循单一约束，对话越长模型准确率下降超11%；同时遵循多个约束时下降超40%；在对话中新增或替换约束时准确率下降超9%。结果表明当前模型在多轮对话中仍难以可靠遵循用户指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令遵循基准大多聚焦于单轮或短多轮场景，缺乏对长程多轮对话中指令遵循能力的评估，因此需要构建相应的评测基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 从真实对话中提取约束，结合模拟的人物驱动交互，构建了SEQUOR自动化基准测试，用于评估模型在长多轮对话中遵循约束的能力。

Result: 1) 单约束场景下，对话越长指令遵循准确率下降超11%；2) 多约束同时遵循时准确率下降超40%；3) 对话中新增或替换约束时准确率下降超9%。

Conclusion: 当前模型在多轮对话中仍难以可靠遵循用户指令，SEQUOR为更好衡量助手的指令遵循能力提供了一种有效方法。

Abstract: In a conversation, a helpful assistant must reliably follow user directives, even as they refine, modify, or contradict earlier requests. Yet most instruction-following benchmarks focus on single-turn or short multi-turn scenarios, leaving open how well models handle long-horizon instruction-following tasks. To bridge this gap, we present SEQUOR, an automatic benchmark for evaluating constraint adherence in long multi-turn conversations. SEQUOR consists of simulated persona-driven interactions built with constraints extracted from real-world conversations. Our results show that even when following a single constraint, instruction-following accuracy consistently decreases as the conversation grows longer, with drops exceeding 11%. This decline becomes larger when models have to follow multiple constraints simultaneously, reducing their accuracy by over 40%. In scenarios where constraints are added or replaced at arbitrary points of the conversation, model accuracy decreases by more than 9%. Taken together, our results reveal that current models still struggle to follow user instructions in multi-turn conversations, and provide a way for better measuring instruction-following capabilities in assistants.

</details>


### [42] [GATHER: Convergence-Centric Hyper-Entity Retrieval for Zero-Shot Cell-Type Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06403)
*Zhonghui Zhang,Feng Jiang,Shaowei Qin,Jiahao Zhao,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GATHER提出了一种面向超实体查询的收敛中心检索器，通过全局多源图遍历识别拓扑收敛节点，在不依赖LLM的情况下从知识图谱中高效检索证据，用于零样本单细胞细胞类型注释。在自建细胞知识图谱上，GATHER以单次LLM调用实现了优于现有KG-RAG基线的准确率（Immune: 27.45%, Lung: 59.64%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于知识图谱的RAG方法采用局部实体级探索策略，从单个基因出发进行迭代推理，但单细胞注释中每个查询包含数十到数百个基因（超实体查询），没有任何单个基因具有决定性，标签仅来自它们的共现模式。这种超实体查询从根本上挑战了局部逐实体探索策略，导致可扩展性差和LLM成本高昂。

Method: 提出GATHER（Graph-Aware Traversal with Hyper-Entity Retrieval），一种面向超实体查询的收敛中心检索器。核心步骤包括：(1) 全局多源图遍历，从多个输入基因同时出发；(2) 识别拓扑收敛节点，即从多个输入基因共同可达的节点；(3) 结合节点重要性和路径重要性评分选择信息性证据，检索过程完全无需LLM参与。并构建了细胞中心生物知识图谱VCKG。

Result: 在Immune和Lung两个数据集上，GATHER分别达到27.45%和59.64%的精确匹配准确率，优于ToG、ToG-2、RoG、PoG等强基线，且仅需每样本单次LLM调用（基线需2-61次调用）。

Conclusion: 收敛节点能够将多实体信号压缩为紧凑、高信息量的证据，每条证据传达的信息量超过多跳路径，为局部逐实体推理提供了高效的全局替代方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot single-cell cell-type annotation aims to determine a cell's type from a given set of expressed genes without any training. Existing knowledge-graph-based RAG approaches retrieve evidence by expanding from source entities and relying on iterative LLM reasoning. However, in this setting each query contains tens to hundreds of genes, where no single gene is decisive and the label emerges only from their collective co-occurrence. Such hyper-entity queries fundamentally challenge local, entity-wise exploration strategies, which reason from individual genes, leading to poor scalability and substantial LLM cost. We propose GATHER (Graph-Aware Traversal with Hyper-Entity Retrieval), a convergence-centric retriever tailored to hyper-entity queries. It performs global multi-source graph traversal and identifies topological convergence points -- nodes jointly reachable from many input genes. These convergence nodes act as high-information hyper-entities that capture entity synergy. By incorporating node- and path-importance scoring, GATHER selects informative evidence entirely without LLM involvement during retrieval. Instantiated on a self-constructed cell-centric biological knowledge graph (VCKG), GATHER outperforms strong KG-RAG baselines (ToG, ToG-2, RoG, PoG) on two datasets (Immune and Lung), achieving the highest exact-match accuracy (27.45% and 59.64%) with only a single LLM call per sample, compared to 2--61 calls for KG-RAG baselines. Our results demonstrate that convergence nodes compress multi-entity signals into compact, high-information evidence that conveys more per item than multi-hop paths, providing an efficient global alternative to local entity-wise reasoning.

</details>


### [43] [MiA-Signature: Approximating Global Activation for Long-Context Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06416)
*Yuqing Li,Jiangnan Li,Mo Yu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文受认知科学中全局点火与意识访问机制的启发，提出了Mindscape Activation Signature（MiA-Signature）概念——一种压缩的全局激活模式表征。在LLM系统中，通过子模选择高维概念来覆盖激活上下文空间，并辅以工作记忆的轻量迭代更新实现。MiA-Signature作为条件信号近似全激活状态的效果，在RAG和智能体系统中均取得了一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学发现，有意识的报告性访问与分布式记忆系统上的全局点火相关联，但个体无法直接访问或枚举所有激活内容。这种张力表明，认知可能依赖一种紧凑表征来近似激活对下游处理的全局影响。受此启发，本文旨在LLM系统中实现类似的压缩全局激活模式。

Method: 提出MiA-Signature概念，通过子模函数选择高维概念以覆盖激活上下文空间，并可选地通过工作记忆进行轻量迭代更新。将MiA-Signature作为条件信号整合到RAG和智能体系统中。

Result: 在多个长上下文理解任务中，将MiA-Signature集成到RAG和智能体系统中均取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: MiA-Signature作为一种计算上可行的近似全激活状态的方法，有效提升了长上下文理解任务的性能，验证了认知科学启发下压缩表征机制在LLM系统中的有效性。

Abstract: A growing body of work in cognitive science suggests that reportable conscious access is associated with \emph{global ignition} over distributed memory systems, while such activation is only partially accessible as individuals cannot directly access or enumerate all activated contents. This tension suggests a plausible mechanism that cognition may rely on a compact representation that approximates the global influence of activation on downstream processing. Inspired by this idea, we introduce the concept of \textbf{Mindscape Activation Signature (MiA-Signature)}, a compressed representation of the global activation pattern induced by a query. In LLM systems, this is instantiated via submodular-based selection of high-level concepts that cover the activated context space, optionally refined through lightweight iterative updates using working memory. The resulting MiA-Signature serves as a conditioning signal that approximates the effect of the full activation state while remaining computationally tractable. Integrating MiA-Signatures into both RAG and agentic systems yields consistent performance gains across multiple long-context understanding tasks.

</details>


### [44] [From 124 Million Tokens to 1,021 Neologisms: A Large-Scale Pipeline for Automatic Neologism Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06426)
*Diego Rossini,Lonneke van der Plas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展、模块化的自动新词检测流水线，结合规则过滤与LLM分类，基于5.27亿条Reddit帖子（2005-2024）提取124.6百万唯一词元，经筛选得到1,021个新词候选，人工验证确认599个（58.7%）为真实词汇创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语料中自动检测新词面临两大挑战：新词的定义范围模糊以及不同LLM对新词分类存在显著分歧，亟需一个可操作、可验证的检测框架。

Method: 采用模块化流水线：先通过基于规则（语法与非语法形态学框架）过滤大规模候选词，再由多个LLM独立分类并通过多数投票决策，最后经人工专家验证确认。

Result: 从5.27亿Reddit帖子的124.6百万唯一词元中筛选出1,021个候选新词（缩减超99.99%），人工验证确认599个（58.7%）为真实新词，同时发现不同LLM间存在显著分类分歧。

Conclusion: 该流水线在架构层面具有模块化和可迁移性，但LLM间的分类不一致凸显了大规模新词检测的操作化难度；代码、词典及标注数据已开源。

Abstract: We present a scalable, modular pipeline for automatic neologism detection that combines rule-based filtering with LLM classification. The pipeline is grounded in two complementary word-formation frameworks, grammatical and extra-grammatical morphology, which jointly define the scope of what counts as a neologism and inform a four-class classification scheme (neologism, entity, foreign, none). While designed to be modular and transferable at the architectural level, the pipeline is instantiated on 527 million English-language Reddit posts spanning 2005-2024. From this corpus, we extract 124.6 million unique tokens and reduce them by over 99.99% to yield 1,021 neologism candidates, a set small enough for manual expert verification. Multiple LLMs independently classify each candidate via majority vote, with a final verification step, revealing substantial cross-model disagreement and highlighting the challenge of operationalizing neologism detection at scale. Manual annotation of all 1,021 candidates confirms that 599 (58.7%) are genuine lexical innovations. The pipeline code, vocabulary compilation scripts, and the annotated candidate list are available at https://github.com/DiegoRossini/neologism-pipeline.

</details>


### [45] [COVID-19 Infodemic. Understanding content features in detecting fake news using a machine learning approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06435)
*Vimala Balakrishnan,Lee Zing Hii,Eric Laporte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索了文本特征（如词二元组、词性分布）和语言特征在假新闻检测中的应用，基于COVID-19疫情期间收集的新数据集，使用决策树、K近邻、逻辑回归、支持向量机和随机森林等传统机器学习方法进行实验。随机森林表现最佳，支持向量机紧随其后。单独使用文本或语言特征均能提升检测效果，但两者结合并未带来显著提升。研究表明传统机器学习方法可有效利用文本和语言特征进行假新闻检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管经验证据表明内容特征（尤其是文本和语言特征）有助于区分真假新闻，但该类特征在假新闻检测中的研究仍不充分，因此本研究旨在系统探究内容特征对假新闻检测的改进作用。

Method: 选取词二元组和词性分布等文本与语言特征，在COVID-19疫情期间收集的新数据集上，使用决策树、K近邻、逻辑回归、支持向量机和随机森林五种传统机器学习分类器进行实验。

Result: 随机森林在所有实验设置中表现最佳，支持向量机紧随其后。单独使用文本特征或语言特征均能提升假新闻检测效果，但两者合并使用未带来显著提升；二元组与词性标签在效果上也存在差异。

Conclusion: 文本和语言特征可成功应用于传统机器学习方法的假新闻检测，无需依赖深度学习；但文本与语言特征的简单组合并不能显著提升检测性能。

Abstract: The use of content features, particularly textual and linguistic for fake news detection is under-researched, despite empirical evidence showing the features could contribute to differentiating real and fake news. To this end, this study investigates a selection of content features such as word bigrams, part of speech distribution etc. to improve fake news detection. We performed a series of experiments on a new dataset gathered during the COVID-19 pandemic and using Decision Tree, K-Nearest Neighbor, Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine and Random Forest. Random Forest yielded the best results, followed closely by Support Vector Machine, across all setups. In general, both the textual and linguistic features were found to improve fake news detection when used separately, however, combining them into a single model did not improve the detection significantly. Differences were also noted between the use of bigrams and part of speech tags. The study shows that textual and linguistic features can be used successfully in detecting fake news using the traditional machine learning approach as opposed to deep learning.

</details>


### [46] [Towards Emotion Consistency Analysis of Large Language Models in Emotional Conversational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06476)
*Sneha Oram,Ojaswita Bhushan,Pushpak Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在情感对话中对其自身生成回应的一致性，发现模型表现低于平均水平，尤其易受中等情感内容中错误预设的影响，且注意力从评估转向生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLMs在情感驱动对话中是否对其自身生成的回应保持一致性，特别是面对包含错误预设的虚假声明查询时的鲁棒性。

Method: 将LLM生成的文本作为查询回馈给同一模型，在极端和中等情绪两个维度上使用三种递增强度的虚假声明查询进行评估。涉及Claude-3.5-haiku、GPT4o-mini和Mistral-7B三个模型，并进行了基于注意力分数的扩展分析。

Result: LLMs表现出低于平均水平的性能，对查询中嵌入的错误信念存在脆弱性，该脆弱性在中等情感内容中尤为显著；注意力分数分析表明模型优先级从评估性转向生成性。

Conclusion: LLMs在情感敏感的高风险场景中部署时需谨慎，因其对自身生成内容的虚假预设缺乏足够的一致性判断能力。

Abstract: In this work, we conduct an analysis to examine the consistency of Large Language Models (LLMs) with respect to their own generated responses in an emotionally-driven conversational context. Specifically, the text generated by LLM is framed as a query to the same model, and its responses are subsequently assessed. This is performed with three queries across two dimensions of extreme and moderate emotions. The three queries are, in particular, false claim queries that contain inherently wrong assumptions (false presuppositions) in increasing order of intensity. Two commercial models, Claude-3.5-haiku, GPT4o-mini, and a medium-sized model, Mistral-7B, are considered in the study. Our findings indicate that LLMs exhibit below-average performance and remain vulnerable to false beliefs embedded within queries. This susceptibility is especially pronounced for moderate emotional content. Furthermore, an extended attention-score-based analysis highlights a shift in models' priority from evaluative to generative. The results raise important considerations for LLMs' deployment in high-stakes, emotionally sensitive contexts.

</details>


### [47] [The Frequency Confound in Language-Model Surprisal and Metaphor Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06506)
*Omar Momen,Sina Zarrieß*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 词频比语言模型惊异度更能预测隐喻新颖性，且惊异度与新颖性的关联在训练早期达到峰值后下降，表明词频可能是主要影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型惊异度与隐喻新颖性判断之间的关系，特别是厘清惊异度与词频在其中的相互作用。

Method: 使用8种Pythia模型规模和154个训练检查点的惊异度估计，结合两种不同的词频度量，分析它们对隐喻新颖性评分的影响。

Result: 词频是比惊异度更强的隐喻新颖性预测因子；惊异度与新颖性的关联在训练早期达到峰值随后下降，这与惊异度-词频关联的同步增强一致。

Conclusion: 以往报道的最佳语言模型惊异度设置可能错误地将语境可预测性与隐喻新颖性和加工难度关联，而词频才是潜在的更主要因素。

Abstract: Language-model (LM) surprisal is widely used as a proxy for contextual predictability and has been reported to correlate with metaphor novelty judgments. However, surprisal is tightly intertwined with lexical frequency. We explore this interaction on metaphor novelty ratings using two different word frequency measures. We analyse surprisal estimates from eight Pythia model sizes and 154 training checkpoints. Across settings, word frequency is a stronger predictor of metaphor novelty than surprisal. Across training stages, the surprisal--novelty association peaks at an early stage and then falls again, mirroring a similarly timed increase in the surprisal--frequency association. These results suggest that the often-reported optimal LM surprisal settings may incorrectly associate contextual predictability with metaphor novelty and processing difficulty, whereas lexical frequency may be the major underlying factor.

</details>


### [48] [STALE: Can LLM Agents Know When Their Memories Are No Longer Valid?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06527)
*Hanxiang Chao,Yihan Bai,Rui Sheng,Tianle Li,Yushi Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STALE基准测试（400个冲突场景、1200个查询），揭示LLM智能体在隐式冲突检测上的严重缺陷——即使最佳模型准确率仅55.2%，并推出CUPMem原型通过结构化状态整合与传播感知搜索初步解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估静态事实检索，忽略了智能体在观察到新证据后需要更新已有信念的能力，特别是"隐式冲突"（新观察否定旧记忆但无显式否定）这一关键失败模式未被充分研究。

Method: 构建包含400个专家验证的冲突场景、1200个评估查询的STALE基准，涵盖100+日常主题和150K token上下文；提出三维探测框架（状态解析、前提抵抗、隐式策略适应）；设计CUPMem原型（结构化状态整合+传播感知搜索）。

Result: 前沿LLM和专用记忆框架最佳准确率仅55.2%，模型常接受用户查询中蕴含的过时假设，且难以识别状态变化对相关记忆的连锁失效。

Conclusion: 检索到更新证据与据此行动之间存在普遍差距，显式状态裁决是构建鲁棒智能体记忆的有前景方向。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to maintain coherent, long-term personalized memory, yet current benchmarks primarily measure static fact retrieval, overlooking the ability to revise stored beliefs when new evidence emerges. We identify a critical and underexplored failure mode, Implicit Conflict: a later observation invalidates an earlier memory without explicit negation, requiring contextual inference and commonsense reasoning to detect. To rigorously evaluate this capability, we introduce STALE, a benchmark of 400 expert-validated conflict scenarios (1,200 evaluation queries across three probing dimensions) spanning over 100 everyday topics with contexts up to 150K tokens. We propose a three-dimensional probing framework that tests State Resolution (detecting that a prior belief is outdated), Premise Resistance (rejecting queries that falsely presuppose a stale state), and Implicit Policy Adaptation (proactively applying updated states in downstream behavior). A systematic evaluation of frontier LLMs and specialized memory frameworks reveals a pervasive gap between retrieving updated evidence and acting on it, with even the best evaluated model achieving only 55.2% overall accuracy. Models often accept outdated assumptions embedded in a user's query, and they struggle to recognize when a change in one aspect of the user's state should invalidate related memories. To establish an initial baseline for state-aware memory, we further present CUPMem, a prototype that strengthens write-time revision through structured state consolidation and propagation-aware search, suggesting that explicit state adjudication is a promising direction for robust agentic memory.

</details>


### [49] [Efficient Pre-Training with Token Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06546)
*Bowen Peng,Théo Gigant,Jeffrey Quesnelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Superposition Training (TST)，一种无需修改模型架构、优化器或并行策略的即插即用预训练方法，通过两个阶段（叠加阶段使用多热交叉熵目标合并连续token，恢复阶段回退标准训练）显著提升数据吞吐量，在10B MoE模型上实现高达2.5倍的预训练时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练在规模扩展时计算效率和吞吐量往往过低，现有提速方法需要对并行策略、优化器或模型架构进行复杂且侵入式的修改，缺乏一种简单、即插即用的高效预训练方案。

Method: TST包含两个阶段：(i) 叠加阶段——将多个连续token合并为一个bag，使用多热交叉熵(MCE)目标进行训练，实现高数据吞吐量；(ii) 恢复阶段——回退到标准训练方式。该方法无需修改并行策略、优化器、分词器、数据或模型架构。

Result: 在270M、600M参数规模以及3B和10B A1B混合专家模型上进行了充分验证，TST在各种设置下均稳健地优于基线损失和下游评估指标；在等损失条件下，10B A1B规模实现了高达2.5倍的预训练总时间缩减。

Conclusion: TST作为一种简单、即插即用的预训练方法，在不修改现有系统组件的前提下显著提升数据吞吐量并缩短训练时间，具有高度的鲁棒性和可扩展性，为大语言模型的高效预训练提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Pre-training of Large Language Models is often prohibitively expensive and inefficient at scale, requiring complex and invasive modifications in order to achieve high data throughput. In this work, we present Token-Superposition Training (TST), a simple drop-in method that significantly improves the data throughput per FLOPs during pre-training without modifying the parallelism, optimizer, tokenizer, data, or model architecture. TST is done in two phases: (i) A highly efficient superposition phase where we combine many contiguous tokens into one bag and train using a multi-hot cross-entropy (MCE) objective, and (ii) a recovery phase where we revert back to standard training. We extensively evaluate TST on the scale of 270M and 600M parameters and validate on 3B and a 10B A1B mixture of experts model, demonstrating that it is highly robust in different settings. Ultimately, TST consistently outperforms baseline loss and downstream evaluations, and under equal-loss settings, TST yields up to a 2.5x reduction in total pre-training time at the 10B A1B scale.

</details>


### [50] [Continuous Latent Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06548)
*Hongcan Guo,Qinyu Zhao,Yian Zhao,Shen Nie,Rui Zhu,Qiushan Guo,Feng Wang,Tao Yang,Hengshuang Zhao,Guoqiang Wei,Yan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Cola DLM是一种层次化潜在扩散语言模型，通过文本VAE学习文本到潜在空间的稳定映射，再利用块因果DiT在连续潜在空间中建模全局语义先验，最后通过条件解码生成文本。其扩散过程执行潜在先验传输而非token级观测恢复，分离了全局语义组织与局部文本实现，在约2B参数规模下验证了强扩展行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在自回归范式下取得成功，但高质量文本生成无需拘泥于固定的从左到右顺序；已有的替代方案在生成效率、可扩展表示学习和有效全局语义建模三者之间仍难以兼顾。

Method: 提出Cola DLM：1) Text VAE学习稳定的文本到潜在映射；2) 块因果DiT在连续潜在空间中建模全局语义先验；3) 条件解码生成文本。扩散过程从统一马尔可夫路径视角执行潜在先验传输，而非token级观测恢复。

Result: 在4个研究问题、8个基准、严格匹配的约2B参数自回归和LLaDA基线以及约2000 EFLOPs的缩放曲线中，Cola DLM验证了其有效配置和强扩展行为。

Conclusion: 层次化连续潜在先验建模是严格token级语言模型的一个原则性替代方案，生成质量和扩展行为可能比似然更能反映模型能力，同时为离散文本与连续模态的统一建模提供了具体路径。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable success under the autoregressive paradigm, yet high-quality text generation need not be tied to a fixed left-to-right order. Existing alternatives still struggle to jointly achieve generation efficiency, scalable representation learning, and effective global semantic modeling. We propose Cola DLM, a hierarchical latent diffusion language model that frames text generation through hierarchical information decomposition. Cola DLM first learns a stable text-to-latent mapping with a Text VAE, then models a global semantic prior in continuous latent space with a block-causal DiT, and finally generates text through conditional decoding. From a unified Markov-path perspective, its diffusion process performs latent prior transport rather than token-level observation recovery, thereby separating global semantic organization from local textual realization. This design yields a more flexible non-autoregressive inductive bias, supports semantic compression and prior fitting in continuous space, and naturally extends to other continuous modalities. Through experiments spanning 4 research questions, 8 benchmarks, strictly matched ~2B-parameter autoregressive and LLaDA baselines, and scaling curves up to about 2000 EFLOPs, we identify an effective overall configuration of Cola DLM and verify its strong scaling behavior for text generation. Taken together, the results establish hierarchical continuous latent prior modeling as a principled alternative to strictly token-level language modeling, where generation quality and scaling behavior may better reflect model capability than likelihood, while also suggesting a concrete path toward unified modeling across discrete text and continuous modalities.

</details>


### [51] [Long Context Pre-Training with Lighthouse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06554)
*Bowen Peng,Subho Ghosh,Jeffrey Quesnelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Lighthouse Attention，一种用于因果Transformer训练的分层对称选择注意力算法，通过可移除的次二次复杂度预处理/后处理步骤和两阶段训练策略，在匹配所有设置条件下实现了更快的训练速度和更低的最终损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列因果Transformer训练受限于标准缩放点积注意力的二次时间和内存复杂度，亟需一种高效且易于集成到现有框架中的训练加速方法。

Method: (i) 次二次复杂度的分层预/后处理步骤，实现序列的自适应压缩与解压缩；(ii) 对称压缩策略，同时池化查询、键和值并保持从左到右的因果性，提升并行性；(iii) 两阶段训练：大部分时间使用Lighthouse Attention预训练，最后通过短时间训练恢复完整注意力模型。分层选择无梯度，无需设计复杂的反向传播核函数。

Result: 在小规模LLM预训练实验中，与全注意力训练相比（所有其他设置匹配），该方法实现了更快的总训练时间，并在恢复阶段后获得更低的最终损失。

Conclusion: Lighthouse Attention作为一种训练专用的可移除注意力加速算法，有效缓解了长序列训练的计算瓶颈，且易于集成到现有SDPA框架中。

Abstract: Training causal transformers at extreme sequence lengths is bottlenecked by the quadratic time and memory of scaled dot-product attention (SDPA). In this work, we propose Lighthouse Attention, a training-only symmetrical selection-based hierarchical attention algorithm that wraps around ordinary SDPA and can be easily removed towards the end of the training. Our hierarchical selection is also gradient-free, which exempts us from dealing with a complicated and potentially inefficient backward pass kernel. Our contribution is three-fold: (i) A subquadratic hierarchical pre- and post-processing step that does adaptive compression and decompression of the sequence. (ii) A symmetrical compression strategy that pools queries, keys and values at the same time, while preserving left-to-right causality, which greatly improves parallelism. (iii) A two stage training approach which we pre-train for the majority of the time with Lighthouse Attention and recover a full attention model at the end with a short training. We run preliminary small scale LLM pre-training experiments that show the effectiveness of our method compared to full attention training with all other settings matched, where we achieve a faster total training time and lower final loss after the recovery phase. Full code is available at: https://github.com/ighoshsubho/lighthouse-attention

</details>

*Yongxin Zhou,Fabien Ringeval,François Portet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了远程认知康复训练中的自动化临床报告生成问题，在无参考报告的低资源场景下，对比了基于规则的模板系统与零样本GPT-4方法，由八位言语治疗师进行九维度评估。结果表明模板系统在流畅性、连贯性和结果呈现上表现更好，而GPT-4更简洁，两者在临床可靠性与语言质量之间存在明确权衡。基于评估反馈，提出了八项临床报告系统设计建议。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知康复治疗需求日益增长，而言语治疗师资源有限，推动了远程康复工具的普及。但这些工具产生的大量交互数据使临床医生难以高效审阅。因此，本文旨在探索在低资源（无参考报告）环境下，如何自动化生成临床报告以减轻医生负担。

Method: 采用两种方法：（1）基于规则的模板系统，将言语治疗领域知识编码为显式决策规则和验证模板；（2）零样本GPT-4方法。两者使用相同的预提取且经专家验证的结构化变量。由八位言语治疗师和应届毕业生采用九指标问卷进行评估。

Result: 模板系统在流畅性、连贯性和结果呈现上得分更高，GPT-4输出更简洁。各评估维度上方向性差异一致，但经多重检验校正后均未达到统计显著性，反映了专家临床评估的规模限制。

Conclusion: 临床可靠性与语言质量之间存在明确权衡。基于评估反馈提出八项临床报告系统设计建议。本研究贡献了一个可复现的方法论，结合了专家启发、分类驱动生成和多维人工评估，适用于低资源场景下的临床自然语言生成，并为生成式AI在医疗中的负责任应用提供了可控比较的范例。

Abstract: The growing demand for cognitive remediation therapy, combined with limited speech therapist availability, has accelerated the adoption of remote rehabilitation tools. These systems generate large volumes of interaction data that are difficult for clinicians to review efficiently. This paper investigates automated clinical report generation for avatar-guided, home-based cognitive remediation sessions in a low-resource setting with no reference reports. We present and compare two approaches: (1) a rule-based template system encoding speech therapy domain knowledge as explicit decision rules and validated templates, ensuring clinical reliability and traceability; and (2) a zero-shot LLM-based approach (GPT-4) aimed at more fluent and concise output. Both systems use identical pre-extracted, expert-validated structured variables, enabling a controlled factual comparison. Outputs were evaluated by eight speech therapists and final-year students using a nine-criterion questionnaire. Results reveal a clear trade-off between clinical reliability and linguistic quality. The template-based system scored higher on fluidity, coherence, and results presentation, while GPT-4 produced more concise output. Directional differences are consistent across evaluation dimensions, though no comparison reached statistical significance after correction, reflecting the scale constraints of expert clinical evaluation. Based on evaluator feedback, we derive eight design recommendations for clinical reporting systems in remote rehabilitation settings. More broadly, this work contributes a replicable methodology combining expert elicitation, taxonomy-driven generation, and multi-dimensional human evaluation for clinical NLG in low-resource settings, and illustrates how controlled comparisons can inform the responsible adoption of generative AI in healthcare.

</details>


### [53] [UniSD: Towards a Unified Self-Distillation Framework for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06597)
*Yiqiao Jin,Yiyang Wang,Lucheng Fu,Yijia Xiao,Yinyi Luo,Haoxin Liu,B. Aditya Prakash,Josiah Hester,Jindong Wang,Srijan Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出UniSD统一框架系统研究自蒸馏（Self-distillation），通过集成多教师一致性、EMA教师稳定化、token级对比学习、特征匹配和散度裁剪等互补机制，在六个基准和六个模型上揭示了自蒸馏的有效条件、关键组件及其交互作用，最终构建的UniSDfull集成管道相比基础模型提升+5.4分，比最强基线提升+2.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏为LLM适配提供了无需强外部教师的可行路径，但自回归LLM中的自蒸馏存在自生成轨迹自由、正确性依赖任务、合理理由仍可能提供不稳定监督等问题；现有方法仅研究孤立的策略选择，其有效性、角色和交互尚不清晰。

Method: 提出UniSD统一框架，集成五种互补机制：多教师一致性（multi-teacher agreement）、EMA教师稳定化（EMA teacher stabilization）、token级对比学习（token-level contrastive learning）、特征匹配（feature matching）和散度裁剪（divergence clipping），系统比较各组件在不同任务中的效果与交互。

Result: 在六个基准、六个模型（来自三个模型家族）上，UniSD揭示了自蒸馏何时优于静态模仿、哪些组件驱动增益以及组件间如何跨任务交互；UniSDfull集成管道实现最强整体性能，相比基础模型提升+5.4分，相比最强基线提升+2.8分。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏是一种实用且可控的LLM适配方法，无需依赖更强的外部教师；UniSD框架为理解自蒸馏组件的作用和交互提供了系统性视角，并证明了集成互补机制的有效性。

Abstract: Self-distillation (SD) offers a promising path for adapting large language models (LLMs) without relying on stronger external teachers. However, SD in autoregressive LLMs remains challenging because self-generated trajectories are free-form, correctness is task-dependent, and plausible rationales can still provide unstable or unreliable supervision. Existing methods mainly examine isolated design choices, leaving their effectiveness, roles, and interactions unclear. In this paper, we propose UniSD, a unified framework to systematically study self-distillation. UniSD integrates complementary mechanisms that address supervision reliability, representation alignment, and training stability, including multi-teacher agreement, EMA teacher stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping. Across six benchmarks and six models from three model families, UniSD reveals when self-distillation improves over static imitation, which components drive the gains, and how these components interact across tasks. Guided by these insights, we construct UniSDfull, an integrated pipeline that combines complementary components and achieves the strongest overall performance, improving over the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points. Extensive evaluation highlights self-distillation as a practical and steerable approach for efficient LLM adaptation without stronger external teachers.

</details>


### [54] [Algospeak, Hiding in the Open: The Trade-off Between Legible Meaning and Detection Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06619)
*Jan Fillies,Ronald E. Robertson,Jeffrey Hancock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文形式化了Algospeak（算法语言）中规避与检测的协同演化动态，提出了“多数可理解调制”（MUM）概念，并构建了一个可复现的框架来生成可调制的、保留语义的Algospeak变体。以COVID-19虚假信息为案例，创建了包含700个调制项的参考数据集，通过七种语言模型进行意义恢复和虚假信息检测两项评估，拟合曲线估计MUM阈值，揭示了可理解性与调制之间的特征关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益介入内容生成与审核，导致Algospeak这类语言规避策略加剧了规避者与检测器之间的协同演化，需要形式化理解其动态机制和可理解性与可检测性之间的权衡关系。

Method: 基于联合行动模型形式化Algospeak动态，提出MUM概念；基于现有分类法构建可调制的、保留语义的Algospeak变体生成框架；以COVID-19虚假信息为例，构建20个基础句×5个调制水平×7种策略的700项调制数据集；用7种语言模型分别进行意义恢复（解释）和虚假信息检测（分类）两项评估；通过曲线拟合估计MUM阈值并进行敏感度分析。

Result: 揭示了可理解性与调制水平之间的特征关系，通过曲线拟合成功估计了多数可理解调制（MUM）阈值，并实现了跨策略和跨模型的敏感度分析。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解Algospeak背后的动态机制奠定了基础，提供了框架、数据集和实验设置，可支撑后续对规避与检测协同演化的深入研究。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate both content generation and moderation, linguistic evasion strategies known as Algospeak have intensified the coevolution between evaders and detectors. This research formalizes the underlying dynamics grounded in a joint action model: when Algospeak increases, detectability and understandability decrease. Further, the concept of Majority Understandable Modulation (MUM) is introduced and defined as the modulation level at which additional evasive alteration increases detector evasion but loses comprehension for the majority of recipients. To empirically probe this trade-off, we introduce a reproducible framework that can be used to create meaning-preserving, Algospeak-style variants, based on an existing taxonomy and with tunable modulation levels. Using COVID-19 disinformation as a first proof-by-example setting, we construct a reference dataset of 700 modulated items, drawn from twenty base sentences across five modulation levels and seven strategies. We then run two linked evaluations with seven different language models: one testing for interpretation through meaning recovery and one for disinformation detection through classification. Curve fitting over modulation levels yields an estimate of the Majority Understandable Modulation threshold and enables sensitivity analyses across strategies and models, see Figure 1. Results reveal the characteristic relationships between understandability and modulation. This study lays the groundwork for understanding the dynamics behind Algospeak and provides the framework, dataset, and experimental setups described.

</details>


### [55] [Cited but Not Verified: Parsing and Evaluating Source Attribution in LLM Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06635)
*Hailey Onweller,Elias Lumer,Austin Huber,Pia Ramchandani,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Corey Feld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对LLM生成Markdown报告中内联引用的来源归因评估框架，使用可复现的AST解析器从三个维度（链接有效性、内容相关性、事实准确性）评估引用。对14个闭源和开源模型的基准测试显示，最先进模型链接有效性>94%、相关性>80%，但事实准确性仅39-77%；且随工具调用次数增加，事实准确性平均下降约42%，揭示检索量增加并不提升引用准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么信任模型自引用（存在偏差），要么使用RAG但不验证源的可访问性、相关性和事实一致性，缺乏对LLM生成报告中内联引用的系统性、可复现的评估框架。

Method: 设计了一种基于AST解析器的自动化评估框架，从LLM生成的Markdown报告中提取内联引用，通过检索实际引用内容，从Link Works（URL可访问性）、Relevant Content（主题对齐）、Fact Check（事实准确性）三个维度评估引用质量，并使用经过人工校准的LLM-as-a-judge评估器。

Result: 14个模型评估显示：最强模型链接有效性>94%、相关性>80%，但事实准确性仅39-77%；半数以上开源模型无法一次生成带引用的报告。消融实验发现，当工具调用从2次增至150次时，事实准确性平均下降约42%。

Conclusion: 面引用质量与事实可靠性之间存在严重脱节，更多检索并不产生更准确的引用；本文框架为评估这一脱节提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) power deep research agents that synthesize information from hundreds of web sources into cited reports, yet these citations cannot be reliably verified. Current approaches either trust models to self-cite accurately, risking bias, or employ retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that does not validate source accessibility, relevance, or factual consistency. We introduce the first source attribution evaluation framework that uses a reproducible AST parser to extract and evaluate inline citations from LLM-generated Markdown reports at scale. Unlike methods that verify claims in isolation, our framework closes the loop by retrieving the actual cited content, enabling human or model evaluators to judge each citation against its source. Citations are evaluated along three dimensions. (1) Link Works verifies URL accessibility, (2) Relevant Content measures topical alignment, and (3) Fact Check validates factual accuracy against source content. We benchmark 14 closed-source and open-source LLMs across three evaluation dimensions using rubric-based LLM-as-a-judge evaluators calibrated through human review. Our results reveal that even the strongest frontier models maintain link validity above 94% and relevance above 80%, yet achieve only 39-77% factual accuracy, while fewer than half of open-source models successfully generate cited reports in a one-shot setting. Ablation studies on research depth show that Fact Check accuracy drops by approximately 42% on average across two frontier models as tool calls scale from 2 to 150, demonstrating that more retrieval does not produce more accurate citations. These findings reveal a critical disconnect between surface-level citation quality and factual reliability, and our framework provides the evaluation infrastructure to assess the disconnect.

</details>


### [56] [StraTA: Incentivizing Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Strategic Trajectory Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06642)
*Xiangyuan Xue,Yifan Zhou,Zidong Wang,Shengji Tang,Philip Torr,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai,Zhenfei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Strategic Trajectory Abstraction (StraTA)，一种通过显式轨迹层策略来增强智能体强化学习的方法，在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld三个基准上显著提升了样本效率和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在长程决策任务中主要采用纯反应式方法，导致探索和信用分配在长轨迹上效果不佳。

Method: StraTA从初始任务状态采样紧凑策略，后续动作基于该策略进行条件化，并通过分层GRPO风格的 rollout 设计联合训练策略生成和动作执行，辅以多样化策略展开和关键自我评判。

Result: 在ALFWorld上达到93.1%成功率，WebShop上达到84.2%成功率，SciWorld上获得63.5%总分，超越前沿闭源模型。

Conclusion: StraTA通过引入显式轨迹层策略显著提升了智能体强化学习的样本效率和最终性能，在多个基准上取得了最优结果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as interactive agents, but optimizing them for long-horizon decision making remains difficult because current methods are largely purely reactive, which weakens both exploration and credit assignment over extended trajectories. In this work, we present Strategic Trajectory Abstraction (StraTA), a simple framework that introduces an explicit trajectory-level strategy into agentic reinforcement learning (RL). StraTA samples a compact strategy from the initial task state, conditions subsequent actions on that strategy, and trains strategy generation and action execution jointly with a hierarchical GRPO-style rollout design, further enhanced by diverse strategy rollout and critical self-judgment. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld show that StraTA consistently improves both sample efficiency and final performance over strong baselines. StraTA reaches success rates of 93.1% on ALFWorld and 84.2% on WebShop. On SciWorld, StraTA attains a 63.5% overall score, outperforming frontier closed-source models.

</details>


### [57] [EMO: Pretraining Mixture of Experts for Emergent Modularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06663)
*Ryan Wang,Akshita Bhagia,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EMO是一种通过文档级专家共享约束训练的MoE模型，使得不同领域的专家子集自然形成领域特异性模块。仅用25%的专家即可接近完整模型性能，实现了大规模稀疏模型的高效模块化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE虽然通过稀疏激活减少计算量，但限制到特定领域的专家子集会严重损害性能，无法在内存受限场景下灵活部署。需要一种能够自然形成可独立使用和组合的专家模块的MoE架构。

Method: EMO在预训练中引入文档级专家共享约束：同一文档内的token必须从同一个共享专家池中选择专家，不同文档可以使用不同的专家池。这个简单约束利用文档边界作为自然语义划分，使相似领域的token倾向于选择相似的专家，从而在无人工先验的情况下自动形成领域特异性的专家分组。

Result: 1B活跃/14B总参数的EMO在1T tokens上预训练后：（1）作为完整模型，性能匹配标准MoE；（2）保留25%（12.5%）的专家仅导致1%（3%）的绝对性能下降，而标准MoE在此设置下完全失效；（3）专家子集在语义层面（如数学、代码等领域）实现专门化，而非标准MoE的低层句法专门化。

Conclusion: EMO证明了通过文档级约束可以在预训练中自组织出领域模块化的专家分组，为大规模稀疏模型的内存高效部署和可组合架构开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models are typically deployed as monolithic systems, requiring the full model even when applications need only a narrow subset of capabilities, e.g., code, math, or domain-specific knowledge. Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs) seemingly offer a potential alternative by activating only a subset of experts per input, but in practice, restricting inference to a subset of experts for a given domain leads to severe performance degradation. This limits their practicality in memory-constrained settings, especially as models grow larger and sparser. We introduce EMO, an MoE designed for modularity-the independent use and composition of expert subsets-without requiring human-defined priors. Our key idea is to encourage tokens from similar domains to rely on similar experts. Since tokens within a document often share a domain, EMO restricts them to select experts from a shared pool, while allowing different documents to use different pools. This simple constraint enables coherent expert groupings to emerge during pretraining using document boundaries alone. We pretrain a 1B-active, 14B-total EMO on 1T tokens. As a full model, it matches standard MoE performance. Crucially, it enables selective expert use: retaining only 25% (12.5%) of experts incurs just a 1% (3%) absolute drop, whereas standard MoEs break under the same setting. We further find that expert subsets in EMO specialize at semantic levels (e.g., domains such as math or code), in contrast to the low-level syntactic specialization observed in standard MoEs. Altogether, our results demonstrate a path toward modular, memory-efficient deployment of large, sparse models and open new opportunities for composable architectures.

</details>

### [58] [Identifier-Free Code Embedding Models for Scalable Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05251)
*Eric Wolos,Michael Doyle*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化了二进制逆向工程中源代码与反编译剥离代码之间的双向函数关联问题，通过对比学习微调Qwen3-Embedding模型，在关联任务上以显著优势超越所有基线模型，并能泛化到未显式训练的常量算法关联任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数关联搜索工具未充分利用AI搜索能力，且已有嵌入模型研究未涵盖源代码与经标准预处理的反编译剥离代码之间的双向关联。

Method: 形式化定义了函数关联问题，采用对比学习微调Qwen3-Embedding模型以提升双向关联性能。

Result: 微调模型在所有函数关联基线上以显著优势超越其他模型，并能泛化到未显式训练的常量算法关联任务。

Conclusion: 通过对比学习微调的Qwen3-Embedding模型能够有效实现源代码与反编译剥离代码的双向函数关联，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Function association is a useful process for binary reverse engineers. Search tools exist to perform association at scale, but they do not utilize the full range of capabilities that AI-enabled search provides. Prior work has explored the development of embedding models for association between certain reverse engineering code representations, but that work does not cover bidirectional association between source code and decompiled, stripped code with standard preprocessing requirements. To bridge this gap, we formalize this function association problem and evaluate the extent to which embedding models can bidirectionally associate between these two representations. To improve model performance at this task, we fine-tune a Qwen3-Embedding model with contrastive learning. We find that our new model outperforms other models on all function association baselines by a substantial margin and generalizes to a constant-algorithm association task it is not explicitly trained on.

</details>


### [59] [SecureMCP: A Policy-Enforced LLM Data Access Framework for AIoT Systems via Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05260)
*Wonbae Kim,Hee-Kyong Yoo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecureMCP 是一个集成RBAC的多层防御框架，用于保护LLM生成的SQL查询在AIoT系统中的安全执行。该框架包含五个防御模块，在IoT-SQL数据集上评估显示：防御模块几乎不影响准确率，对2400个对抗性查询实现了82.3%的策略合规性，真实防御失败率仅3.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的SQL查询在AIoT系统中面临严重的prompt注入攻击风险，现有NL2SQL研究只关注查询准确率，MCP服务器仅提供SQL层面保护而缺乏细粒度角色访问控制。

Method: 提出SecureMCP框架，集成RBAC与MCP服务器，构建五模块顺序故障闭合流水线：check_policy（表列级RBAC）、explain_gate（高成本查询阻断）、SQL Interceptor（危险模式检测）、Risk Level Filter（SQL风险分类）、DB Isolation（跨库限制），映射到OWASP Top 10的六种prompt注入类型。

Result: 在IoT-SQL数据集上，执行准确率保持在65.1%-76.4%（基线63.8%）；对抗性查询策略合规性82.3%，真实防御失败率3.4%；check_policy贡献78.7%的阻断，次级模块额外提升17.5个百分点；注入契合率72.5%证实LLM高度脆弱性。

Conclusion: SecureMCP通过外部策略执行有效弥补了LLM自身防护不足的缺陷，多层防御架构在保持查询准确性的同时显著提升了对抗prompt注入攻击的鲁棒性，证明了在AIoT场景下部署策略驱动的数据访问控制的必要性。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Model (LLM)-generated SQL queries in Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) systems introduces critical security risks, as prompt injection attacks can manipulate LLMs into producing unauthorized queries that expose sensitive data or execute destructive operations. Existing NL2SQL research focuses on query accuracy, while MCP server implementations provide only SQL-level protections without fine-grained role-based access control. This paper proposes SecureMCP, a policy-enforced LLM data access framework integrating Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) with an MCP server to establish multi-layer defense for LLM-generated SQL execution. The framework incorporates five defense modules -- check_policy for table-and-column-level RBAC, explain_gate for cost-explosive query blocking, SQL Interceptor for dangerous pattern detection, Risk Level Filter for SQL risk classification, and DB Isolation for cross-database restriction -- operating in a sequential fail-closed pipeline mapped to six prompt injection types grounded in the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications. We evaluate SecureMCP on the IoT-SQL dataset (11 tables, 173 columns, 239,398 records) using Qwen3-8B. Experiment A demonstrates that defense modules preserve execution accuracy, with EX-in-ALLOW remaining within 65.1%-76.4% across four RBAC roles, matching the unprotected baseline of 63.8%. Experiment B shows that SecureMCP achieves 82.3% Policy Compliance on 2,400 adversarial queries, with genuine defense failures limited to 3.4%. The defense-in-depth analysis reveals check_policy accounts for 78.7% of blocks, while secondary modules contribute an additional 17.5 percentage-point improvement. The Injection Incorporation Rate of 72.5% confirms high LLM susceptibility, establishing the necessity of external policy enforcement.

</details>


### [60] [Differential Privacy in the Extensive-Form Bandit Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05266)
*Stephen Pasteris,Rahul Savani,Theodore Turocy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展式赌博机问题，提出了一种满足ε-局部差分隐私的算法，实现了$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$的遗憾界，其中A是动作总数，S是约简策略数，T是试验次数。该算法每轮的时间复杂度与服务器向用户传输约简策略所需的时间相当。这是首个在扩展式赌博机问题中研究差分隐私的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展式赌博机问题中，学习者（由服务器协调的用户）在每轮试验中与环境交互并观察收益/损失，但这一过程中可能暴露用户的隐私信息。目前缺乏在扩展式赌博机问题中研究差分隐私的保护机制，且局部差分隐私作为最强隐私保护形式尚未被探索。

Method: 提出一种满足ε-局部差分隐私的算法，在每轮试验中，服务器向用户传输约简策略，用户根据该策略执行动作并观察结果。算法的遗憾界为$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$，每轮时间复杂度（除去对数因子）等于传输约简策略所需的时间。

Result: 算法实现了$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$的遗憾界，其中A为动作总数，S为约简策略数，T为试验次数。时间复杂度与策略传输时间相当。

Conclusion: 这是首个在扩展式赌博机问题中研究差分隐私的工作，所提出的算法在满足ε-局部差分隐私（最强差分隐私版本）的同时，取得了次线性遗憾界，证明了隐私保护与学习效率可以兼得。

Abstract: We consider the extensive-form bandit problem, where on each trial the learner (a user coordinated by a server) plays an extensive-form game against an oblivious adversary, observing the information sets it finds itself in as well as the resulting payoff/loss. We give an algorithm for this problem that satisfies $ε$-local differential privacy and attains a regret of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$, where $A$ is the total number of actions that the learner can possibly take, $S$ is the number of the learner's possible reduced strategies, and $T$ is the number of trials. On each trial, the time complexity of our algorithm is, up to a factor logarithmic in the maximum number of actions at an infoset, equal to the time required for the server to transmit the reduced strategy to the user. We note that local differential privacy is the strongest version of differential privacy and, to the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study differential privacy of any form in the extensive-form bandit problem.

</details>


### [61] [Dynamic Authorization for Knowledge-Base Agents in 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05269)
*Loay Abdelrazek,Leyli Karacay,Marin Orlic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对6G多智能体系统中知识库的访问控制问题，提出了一种结合角色和一阶逻辑谓词的混合授权框架，在图谱三元组级别实施零信任原则，消除了权限继承。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G架构向去中心化多智能体系统演进，传统RBAC模型无法满足自主智能体对基于语义的知识库进行细粒度安全访问的需求。

Method: 提出一种混合授权框架，将角色与一阶逻辑谓词相结合，在三元组（主语-谓语-宾语）级别实施零信任授权，消除权限继承机制。

Result: 实现了知识图谱级别的细粒度访问控制，确保智能体仅能访问其特定功能生命周期所需的元数据。

Conclusion: 该混合授权框架能够满足6G去中心化MAS环境中对语义数据的安全访问需求，相比传统RBAC提供了更精细的授权粒度。

Abstract: As 6G architectures transition toward decentralized Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), ensuring secure access to shared Knowledge Bases (KB) is critical. Traditional authorization models like RBAC fail to provide the granularity required for autonomous agents interacting with Semantic-based data. This work proposes a hybrid authorization framework that integrates roles and First-Order Logic (FOL) predicates to enforce zero-trust principles at the knowledge-graph level. We eliminate permission inheritance by enforcing authorization at the triple level (Subject-Predicate-Object), ensuring agents only access metadata required for their specific functional lifecycle.

</details>


### [62] [Shattering the Echo Chamber: Hidden Safeguards in Manuscripts Against the AI Takeover of Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05271)
*Oubo Ma,Ruixiao Lin,Jiahao Chen,Yuan Su,Yong Yang,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出IntraGuard，一种基于PDF结构-视觉解耦的黑盒防御框架，通过在PDF底层结构中嵌入异构防御文本对象，有效防御端到端审稿外包威胁，在7个商业聊天机器人和12个 venues 上实现最高84%的防御成功率，且不影响人类审稿人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力提升，编辑委员会和程序委员会越来越担心审稿人完全外包同行评审给商业聊天机器人，现有防御方法（在稿件中嵌入隐藏指令）因依赖同质载荷且采用流间注入方式，容易被清理或中和，效果脆弱。

Method: 提出IntraGuard防御框架，核心思想是利用PDF的结构-视觉解耦特性，在PDF底层结构中通过三种流内注入机制嵌入异构防御文本对象（不改变视觉呈现），支持显式策略（触发拒绝或警告信号）和隐式策略（嵌入预定义文本标记）。

Result: 在7个真实商业聊天机器人设置和12个跨学科 venues 上，防御成功率高达84%，对人类审稿人保持同行评审不变性，在普通个人电脑上平均开销仅每篇稿件1秒，并评估了11种自适应攻击。

Conclusion: IntraGuard是一种轻量级、硬件无关的防御框架，能有效应对端到端审稿外包威胁，并讨论了构建集成防御的启示。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly capable, editorial boards and program committees are growing concerned about reviewers who fully outsource peer review to commercial chatbots. This concern stems from prior findings that current chatbots lack the independent critical thinking and depth of reasoning required to assess scientific novelty. One promising direction for mitigating this concern is to embed hidden instructions into manuscripts that disrupt or alter chatbot-generated reviews. However, existing methods remain intuitive and fragile, as they typically rely on homogeneous payloads injected in an inter-stream manner, rendering them susceptible to sanitization or neutralization.
  In this paper, we identify End-to-End Review Outsourcing as an emerging threat and propose IntraGuard, a black-box, venue-agnostic defense framework grounded in the structural--visual decoupling inherent to the PDF. Designed for committee-side deployment, IntraGuard supports both explicit strategies that trigger refusal or warning signals, and implicit strategies that embed predefined textual markers into the generated review. These strategies can be deployed via any of three intra-stream injection mechanisms, each of which seamlessly embeds heterogeneous defensive text objects within the PDF's underlying structure without altering its visual presentation. Extensive evaluations across 7 real-world commercial chatbot settings and 12 venues spanning diverse disciplines show that IntraGuard achieves a defense success rate of up to 84%, while preserving peer-review invariance for human reviewers. IntraGuard is lightweight and hardware-independent, incurring an average overhead of only one second per manuscript on a commodity personal computer. We further evaluate 11 adaptive attacks spanning manuscript sanitization and instruction interference, and discuss the implications of constructing ensemble defenses.

</details>


### [63] [Sealing the Audit-Runtime Gap for LLM Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05274)
*Tingda Shen,Yebo Feng,Konglin Zhu,Xiaojun Jia,Yang Liu,Lin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SIGIL是一个用于LLM技能的可验证安全框架，通过去中心化链上注册表、DAO审核委员会和技能验证加载器（SVL），将技能的审计与运行时执行进行密码学绑定，防止供应链攻击。实验在1023个真实技能上验证，批量验证时间低于86毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生态系统（如Claude Code、ChatGPT）依赖技能（指令+可执行工具），但技能内容与可信指令无法可靠分离，可执行方能调用特权操作，导致供应链面临注入、篡改、抽地毯攻击。现有防御方案存在审计与运行时的割裂问题，缺乏端到端可验证性。

Method: SIGIL提出双阶段方案：(1) 可验证托管：通过防篡改的去中心化链上注册表直接服务技能获取，支持四种发布类型（透明、许可、密封、承诺），所有技能需经DAO审核委员会（质押-罚没经济模型下的可插拔审计）批准；(2) 可验证加载：技能验证加载器（SVL）作为强制加载路径，执行检索、解密、完整性校验及权限清单强制注入。

Result: 在实际部署中对1023个真实技能（涵盖六种攻击类型）进行测试，SVL完成批量完整性验证的时间低于86毫秒，证明密码学绑定从发布到运行时的方案在实践成本上是可行的。

Conclusion: SIGIL首次弥合了LLM技能审计与运行时的间隙，实现了从发布到运行时全链路的密码学可验证安全绑定，且性能开销在实用范围内。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) ecosystems such as Claude Code and ChatGPT increasingly rely on skills: packages of natural-language instructions and executable tools. Once in the LLM's context, skill content cannot be reliably separated from trusted instructions, and a skill's executable side can invoke privileged actions, exposing the skill supply chain to injection, tampering, and rug-pull attacks. Existing defenses are stage-bound: centralized signing, audit reports unbound from the runtime artifact, or policy engines that cannot attest to what was approved. We present SIGIL, the first framework that seals the audit-runtime gap for LLM skills. SIGIL delivers verifiable hosting through a tamper-evident, decentralized on-chain registry from which LLMs fetch skills directly. The registry admits four publication types, Transparent, Licensed, Sealed, and Committed, spanning plaintext public distribution, monetized access, custodial use, and off-chain workflows; before admission, every skill is vetted by a Decentralized Autonomous Organization (DAO) audit committee that supports pluggable auditing methods under a stake-and-slash economic model. At load time, SIGIL delivers verified loading through a skill verification protocol executed by a Skill Verification Loader (SVL) embedded as the mandatory loading path: the SVL retrieves and decrypts the skill as its type requires, verifies its integrity against the on-chain record, and enforces its permission manifest before context injection. We evaluate SIGIL on a real-world deployment against 1,023 in-the-wild skills spanning six attack types. At load time, the SVL verifies each skill's integrity against its on-chain record and enforces its approved permission manifest, completing batched verification under 86 ms. Together, these results show that LLM skills can be cryptographically bound from publication through runtime at practical cost.

</details>


### [64] [GLiNER Guard: Unified Encoder Family for Production LLM Safety and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05277)
*Bogdan Minko,Sabrina Sadiekh,Evgeniy Kokuykin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GLiNER Guard (GLiGuard)，一种统一的编码器模型，可在单次前向传播中同时完成安全分类和PII检测。紧凑型模型（145-147M）在单A100上达到193 req/s，P99延迟低于1秒，吞吐量比GLiNER2高1.6倍；Omni版本（209M）在安全基准上与大型模型竞争。同时发布PII-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级LLM系统需要在严格的延迟和成本约束下同时进行安全审核和PII检测，而现有方案存在矛盾：自回归审核器准确但昂贵，轻量级编码器更快但能力不足。

Method: 提出GLiGuard，一种统一编码器架构，在单次前向传播中同时完成安全分类和PII检测。包含三种变体：紧凑型单/双编码器（145-147M）用于高吞吐服务，以及GLiGuard Omni（209M）用于更强审核质量。

Result: 紧凑型模型在动态批处理下单A100达到193 req/s，P99延迟低于1秒，吞吐量比GLiNER2高1.6倍；Omni在公共安全基准上与更大的审核模型竞争。

Conclusion: 基于编码器的护栏模型是始终在线审核场景下低成本、实用的替代方案。

Abstract: Production LLM systems require both safety moderation and PII detection under strict latency and cost constraints. This creates a trade-off: autoregressive moderators are accurate but expensive, while lightweight encoders are faster but less capable. We present GLiNER Guard (GLiGuard), a unified encoder that performs safety classification and PII detection in a single forward pass, simplifying safety pipelines. We introduce three variants: compact uni- and bi-encoders (145-147M) for high-throughput serving, and GLiGuard Omni (209M) for stronger moderation quality. Under dynamic batching on a single A100, the compact model reaches 193 requests/sec with P99 latency below 1s, achieving 1.6x higher throughput than GLiNER2. Omni remains competitive with much larger moderators on public safety benchmarks. We also release PII-Bench, a span-level benchmark for evaluating PII detection in end-to-end pipelines. Overall, encoder-based guardrails offer a practical low-cost alternative for always-on moderation. Models and benchmarks are released on HuggingFace.

</details>


### [65] [Securing the Agent: Vendor-Neutral, Multitenant Enterprise Retrieval and Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05287)
*Francisco Javier Arceo,Varsha Prasad Narsing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对多租户企业环境中RAG和Agent系统存在的“相关性≠授权”安全漏洞，提出了一种分层隔离架构（策略感知的摄取、检索时门控、共享推理和服务端编排），并通过开源框架OGX验证了该架构在消除跨租户数据泄露方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实企业环境中的RAG和Agent系统面临多租户、数据异构、严格的访问控制、合规性及成本压力等挑战，但现有检索系统按相关性而非授权排序文档，导致查询可能泄露其他租户的机密数据。此外，工具中介的披露、跨轮对话的上下文积累以及客户端编排绕过等问题进一步加剧了安全风险。

Method: 提出分层隔离架构：(1) 策略感知的摄取阶段；(2) 检索时的ABAC门控机制；(3) 共享推理与服务端Agent编排，将工具执行授权、状态隔离和策略强制执行集中在服务端，形成天然的隔离执行点。

Result: 通过开源框架OGX实现并验证，ABAC门控机制在消除跨租户数据泄露的同时，引入的额外开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 服务端编排的分层隔离架构能有效解决多租户环境中的授权与检索矛盾，在保障安全的同时保持高性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic AI systems are increasingly prevalent in enterprise AI deployments. However, real enterprise environments introduce challenges largely absent from academic treatments and consumer-facing APIs: multiple tenants with heterogeneous data, strict access-control requirements, regulatory compliance, and cost pressures that demand shared infrastructure.
  A fundamental problem underlies existing RAG architectures in these settings: retrieval systems rank documents by relevance--whether through semantic similarity, keyword matching, or hybrid approaches--not by authorization, so a query from one tenant can surface another tenant's confidential data simply because it scores highest. We formalize this gap and analyze additional shortcomings--including tool-mediated disclosure, context accumulation across turns, and client-side orchestration bypass--that arise when agentic systems conflate relevance with authorization. To address these challenges, we introduce a layered isolation architecture combining policy-aware ingestion, retrieval-time gating, and shared inference, enforced through server-side agentic orchestration. This approach centralizes security-critical operations--tool execution authorization, state isolation, and policy enforcement--on the server, creating natural enforcement points for multitenant isolation while allowing client-side frameworks to retain control over agent composition and latency-sensitive operations.
  We validate the proposed architecture through an open-source implementation in OGX, a vendor-neutral framework that implements an OpenAI-compatible, open-source Responses API with server-side multi-turn orchestration. We evaluate it empirically and show that ABAC gating eliminates cross-tenant leakage while introducing negligible overhead.

</details>


### [66] [How Far Are VLMs from Privacy Awareness in the Physical World? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05340)
*Junran Wang,Xinjie Shen,Zehao Jin,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ImmersedPrivacy，一个基于Unity的交互式音视频评估框架，用于测试VLM在物理环境中的隐私意识。通过对12个SOTA模型的评估，发现所有模型在杂乱场景中存在感知缺陷，在社交情境转换中准确率不超过65%，在冲突指令下最优模型仅51%的情况能平衡任务与隐私。结论是当前VLM存在感知脆弱性，无法将隐私知识转化为情境行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私评估基准局限于单模态、基于文本的表征，无法捕捉真实物理环境中的隐私挑战。随着VLM被部署为具身助手的认知核心，它们能够在家庭、医院等私密空间中观察和操作隐私敏感信息，亟需一个能够模拟真实物理环境的评估框架。

Method: 构建基于Unity模拟器的交互式音视频评估框架ImmersedPrivacy，设置三个递进层级：1) 在杂乱场景中识别敏感物品；2) 适应社交情境变化；3) 解决显式指令与隐式隐私约束之间的冲突。评估12个SOTA模型。

Result: 所有模型在杂乱场景中性能随复杂度增加单调下降；社交情境变化下无模型超过65%选择准确率；冲突指令下最佳模型gemini-3.1-pro仅51%案例能完美平衡任务完成与隐私保护。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在物理世界中存在感知脆弱性，无法让隐私知识引导其情境行为，暗示需要从感知能力与知识推理两个维度共同改进。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous cognitive cores for embodied assistants, evaluating their privacy awareness in physical environments becomes critical. Unlike digital chatbots, these agents operate in intimate spaces, such as homes and hospitals, where they possess the physical agency to observe and manipulate privacy-sensitive information and artifacts. However, current benchmarks remain limited to unimodal, text-based representations that cannot capture the demands of real-world settings. To bridge this gap, we present ImmersedPrivacy, an interactive audio-visual evaluation framework that simulates realistic physical environments using a Unity-based simulator. ImmersedPrivacy evaluates physically grounded privacy awareness across three progressive tiers that test a model's ability to identify sensitive items in cluttered scenes, adapt to shifting social contexts, and resolve conflicts between explicit commands and inferred privacy constraints. Our evaluation of 12 state-of-the-art models reveals consistent deficits. In cluttered scenes, all models exhibit monotonic performance decay as scene complexity grows due to perceptual deficit. When social context shifts, no model exceed 65% selection accuracy. Under conflicting commands, the best model gemini-3.1-pro perfectly balances task completion and privacy preservation in only 51% of cases. These findings reveal that current VLMs in the physical world suffer from perceptual fragility and fail to let their knowledge of privacy cues govern their situated behavior. Our code and data is available at https://github.com/immersed-privacy/immersed-privacy .

</details>


### [67] [Privacy Without Losing Place: A Paradigm for Private Retrieval in Spatial RAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05459)
*Kennedy Edemacu,Mohammad Mahdi Shokri,Vinay M. Shashidhar,Jong Wook Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PAS（隐私锚点替换）机制，通过在RAG系统中用相对锚点编码替代用户真实位置来实现位置隐私保护，在合成数据集上取得约370-400m的对抗定位误差，同时保留一半以上检索性能，且下游生成质量保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私方法直接扰动用户位置，难以与现代RAG管道无缝集成，需要一种结构化的位置隐私机制来兼顾隐私保护与检索生成性能。

Method: PAS使用相对锚点编码表示位置，包含锚点、方向区间和距离区间三个分量，将用户位置替换为结构化的锚点表示而非直接扰动原始坐标。

Result: 在合成城市数据集上，PAS实现了约370-400m的对抗定位误差，保留了超过一半的基线检索性能，且下游生成质量保持稳健。隐私-效用关系呈现非单调特性。

Conclusion: 结构化空间表示（如PAS）为RAG系统中的位置推理提供了一种实用的隐私保护方法，大语言模型能够补偿不完美的空间检索，且锚点离散化导致的几何偏差使隐私-效用关系区别于传统的连续噪声机制。

Abstract: This work introduces PAS -- Privacy Anchor Substitution, a structured mechanism for enabling user location privacy in spatial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems. Unlike conventional differential privacy methods that directly perturb user locations, PAS represents location with relative anchor encoding consisting of an anchor, direction bin, and distance bin, allowing seamless integration with modern RAG pipelines. We evaluate PAS on a synthetic urban dataset and show that it achieves impressive coarse privacy guarantees, with approximately 370-400m adversarial location error, while retaining more than half of the baseline retrieval performance. Despite the slight drop in retrieval performance, the downstream generation quality under PAS remains comparatively robust, indicating that large language models can compensate for imperfect spatial retrieval. Furthermore, we provide empirical analysis showing that PAS exhibits non-monotonic privacy-utility relationship with respect to privacy parameters. We attribute this to geometric bias induced by anchor discretization, making it different from continuous noise mechanisms such as geo-indistinguishability. Our results show that structured spatial representations offer a practical approach to privacy in location based reasoning in RAG systems.

</details>


### [68] [SOCpilot: Verifying Policy Compliance for LLM-Assisted Incident Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05501)
*Sidnei Barbieri,Leonardo Vaz de Meneses,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SOCpilot是一个用于验证LLM作为安全运营中心（SOC）副驾驶所生成的应急响应计划合规性的框架。它通过定义事件包、行动目录、策略规则、验证器和公共证据表面，在计划边界处衡量合规性。在金融行业200个真实事件上的评估表明，确定性验证器移除了466个不合规的审批门控行动，且未降低基线任务召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心开始使用大语言模型作为副驾驶来起草应急响应计划，但这些计划虽然行动目录有效，却可能违反强制步骤、必要顺序或审批门控。现有方法无法在计划边界处系统性地衡量这种合规性问题。

Method: 提出SOCpilot框架，固定事件包、行动目录、策略规则、验证器和公共证据表面，通过确定性验证器验证LLM副驾驶提出的行动轨迹合规性。在金融行业200个真实事件上评估两个LLM提供商，与分析师编写的参考计划进行对比。

Result: 确定性验证器移除了466个不合规的审批门控行动，且未降低基线任务召回率。相同的内联策略文本对两个LLM提供商产生了相反方向的影响。聚合率在固定语料库的3次重复运行中保持稳定。

Conclusion: SOCpilot能够有效检测LLM副驾驶生成的应急响应计划中的合规性问题，为LLM辅助安全运营提供了可衡量的合规性验证框架。发布的工允许独立审查者在无需访问私有事件数据的情况下重现公开结果。

Abstract: Security operations centers (SOCs) are beginning to use large language models (LLMs) as copilots to draft incident-response plans. These plans may include actions that are valid per the catalog but still violate mandatory steps, required ordering, or approval gates before analyst review. SOCpilot makes this compliance question measurable at the plan boundary. It fixes the incident package, action catalog, policy rules, verifier, and public evidence surface. Next, it verifies the copilot's proposed action trace. We evaluate two LLM providers on 200 real incidents from an anonymized production SOC in a financial-sector case study. We compare their plans to paired analyst-authored references from the same security orchestration, automation, and response (SOAR) cases. An identical inline policy text moves the two providers in opposite directions. A deterministic verifier removes 466 non-compliant, approval-gated actions, without reducing baseline-task recall. Aggregate rates remain stable across 3 reruns of the fixed corpus. The official evidence focuses on approval-gated decisions regarding recovery and containment. Separately, the artifact exposes zero-cost readiness checks for mandatory and ordering repairs. We release the runnable artifact so independent reviewers can rederive the public results without access to private incident data.

</details>


### [69] [Beyond Collection: Measuring the Detection Efficacy of Modern Security Logging Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05531)
*Ryan Holeman,John Hastings,Varghese Mathew Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了自动化安全漏洞利用遥测收集（SETC）框架，首次系统性地评估了CIM、OCSF和ECS三种现代安全日志标准在检测50个远程代码执行漏洞时的有效性，量化了各标准的遥测完整性和漏洞可检测性，为安全从业者提供了基于证据的日志标准选用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对主流日志标准（CIM、OCSF、ECS）在安全检测有效性方面的系统比较，安全从业者在选择日志标准时缺乏充分的证据支撑。

Method: 提出SETC（Security Exploit Telemetry Collection）框架，在容器化环境中自动生成可复现的漏洞利用场景，收集跨多种日志标准的遥测数据，并通过遥测完整性和漏洞可检测性两个指标量化各日志标准的检测效能。

Result: 基于50个不同的远程代码执行漏洞的实验，发现各日志标准在捕获关键攻击指标方面存在显著差异，揭示了关键的安全缺口。

Conclusion: 提供了一种可扩展、可复现的漏洞利用遥测评估方法，为安全从业者选择日志标准提供了明确的、基于证据的指导。

Abstract: Effective security logging is crucial for the timely and accurate detection of cyber threats; however, the relative effectiveness of various industry-standard logging frameworks remains understudied. This paper addresses this critical gap by presenting the first systematic evaluation of modern security logging standards utilizing a novel methodology built upon the automated Security Exploit Telemetry Collection (SETC) framework. SETC systematically generates reproducible exploit scenarios in containerized environments, collecting rich telemetry across multiple logging standards, including CIM (Common Information Model), OCSF (Open Cybersecurity Schema Framework), and ECS (Elastic Common Schema). The detection efficacy of each logging standard is quantified by measuring telemetry completeness and exploit detectability across standardized logs through detailed experiments involving 50 diverse remote code execution vulnerabilities. The resulting findings identify critical gaps and reveal significant differences in logging standards' abilities to capture key attack indicators. Our contributions include a novel evaluation methodology that enables scalable and reproducible analysis of exploit telemetry, as well as new findings that provide clear, evidence-based guidance for security practitioners to make informed decisions about adopting logging standards.

</details>


### [70] [Architecture Matters: Comparing RAG Systems under Knowledge Base Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05632)
*Samuel Korn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了四种RAG架构在对抗性知识库投毒下的鲁棒性，发现架构是影响鲁棒性的关键变量：攻击成功率从Vanilla RAG的81.9%到RLM的24.4%，差距达58个百分点。对抗性框架（而非检索优化）是攻击效果的主要驱动力，而矛盾检测能力并不能保证可靠解决矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对RAG系统的知识库投毒攻击研究几乎只评估了vanilla retrieve-then-generate流水线，而更先进的架构（如多智能体辩论、智能检索、递归语言模型）在面对对抗性优化的矛盾信息时的鲁棒性尚未被测试。

Method: 在921个Natural Questions QA对上，对四种RAG架构（Vanilla RAG、Agentic RAG、MADAM-RAG、Recursive Language Models）进行单文档（N=1）投毒控制实验，比较干净基线、朴素注入和CorruptRAG-AK（一种针对可信度评估的元认知对抗攻击）的效果。引入七类行为分类法分析模型输出。

Result: CorruptRAG-AK下攻击成功率从Vanilla的81.9%到RLM的24.4%，跨架构差距近58个百分点；干净准确率均约92%。对抗性框架而非检索优化驱动了CorruptRAG-AK的优势。MADAM-RAG矛盾检测率最高但精度仅~48.5%，且在干净输入上产生41.4%的无回答率。

Conclusion: 不同RAG架构在对抗性投毒下的鲁棒性差异巨大，脆弱性主要集中在内容推理阶段而非检索阶段。矛盾检测不等于可靠解决，引入细粒度行为分类有助于更全面评估模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are vulnerable to knowledge base poisoning, yet existing attacks have been evaluated almost exclusively against vanilla retrieve-then-generate pipelines. Architectures designed to handle conflicting retrieved information - multi-agent debate, agentic retrieval, recursive language models - remain untested against adversarially optimized contradictions. We evaluate four RAG architectures (vanilla RAG, agentic RAG, MADAM-RAG, and Recursive Language Models) under controlled single-document (N=1) poisoning on 921 Natural Questions QA pairs, comparing a clean baseline, naive injection, and CorruptRAG-AK - an adversarial attack whose meta-epistemic framing targets credibility assessment. Architecture is a high-impact variable in adversarial robustness: under CorruptRAG-AK, attack success rates range from 81.9% (vanilla) to 24.4% (RLM) - a spread of nearly 58 percentage points across architectures with comparable clean accuracy (~92%). Decomposing this gap, once the poisoned document is retrieved, adversarial framing - not retrieval optimization - drives the majority of CorruptRAG-AK's advantage for three of four architectures, localizing the cross-architecture vulnerability at the content-reasoning stage. Our MADAM-RAG reimplementation shows the highest apparent contradiction detection rate, though our LLM judge over-identifies this behavior (~48.5% precision), so reported rates are upper bounds. Regardless of detection, MADAM-RAG cannot resolve contradictions reliably, producing a 41.4% non-answer rate even on clean inputs - though implementation divergences from the original may contribute. We introduce a seven-category behavioral taxonomy capturing contradiction detection, hedging, and failure modes beyond binary accuracy. Code, data, and analysis notebooks are publicly available.

</details>


### [71] [AoI-Guided Client Selection for Robust and Timely Federated Intrusion Detection in Cloud-Edge Security Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05644)
*Chun Yin Chiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在云-边入侵检测中的客户端参与问题，将信息年龄(AoI)作为时效性指标，提出了三种轻量级客户端选择策略(AoI优先、效用优先、混合策略)。实验表明，AoI感知的选择策略相比随机采样可降低约39-41%的平均AoI和约70%的峰值AoI，混合策略在保持检测性能的同时提供了时效性与质量的平衡调节机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云-边协同的入侵检测场景中，联邦学习面临客户端子集参与、异构带宽、落后节点和掉线等问题，导致服务器依赖过时的客户端信息。现有研究缺乏从时效性角度系统研究客户端参与策略对检测性能的影响，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1. 将信息年龄(AoI)引入联邦入侵检测，定义客户端信息的时效性度量；2. 提出三种轻量级客户端选择策略：AoI优先(选择信息最旧的客户端)、效用优先(选择对模型更新贡献最大的客户端)、混合AoI+效用策略(可调节权衡参数)；3. 在多个数据集(CIC-IDS2017、NSL-KDD、ToN-IoT、合成漂移基准)上进行实验，涵盖干净、投毒和投毒+鲁棒聚合三种设置。

Result: AoI感知选择策略相比随机采样降低平均AoI约39-41%、峰值AoI约70%；混合策略在保持Macro-F1/AUC性能的同时提供了可解释的权衡调节，但在加入假阳性率指标后并非严格帕累托最优；将AoI引导选择与修剪均值聚合结合可抵御标签翻转投毒攻击。

Conclusion: 云-边隐私保护的入侵检测分析可以通过轻量级调度层改善时效性，而无需改变底层联邦学习的参与预算；AoI引导的客户端选择策略本身并非独立的拜占庭防御机制，但可与鲁棒聚合方法结合使用。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is attractive for cloud-edge intrusion detection because it enables collaborative training over distributed telemetry without centralizing raw logs. In production security analytics pipelines, however, only a subset of clients participates in each round, and heterogeneous bandwidth, stragglers, and dropouts can cause the server to rely on stale client information. This paper studies client participation as a timeliness-aware systems problem using Age of Information (AoI). We compare three lightweight policies for federated intrusion detection: AoI-first, utility-first, and a hybrid AoI+utility rule with a tunable trade-off parameter.
  Across a CIC-IDS2017 DDoS/PortScan mini subset, NSL-KDD, ToN-IoT, and a synthetic drift benchmark under clean, poisoning, and poisoning-plus-robust-aggregation settings, AoI-aware selection reduces average AoI by about 39--41% and peak AoI by about 70% relative to random sampling while keeping the per-round communication budget fixed. The hybrid policy usually preserves Macro-F1/AUC and provides an interpretable knob for balancing freshness, detection quality, and robustness, although it is not uniformly Pareto-dominant once false positive rate is included. Robustness is evaluated by combining AoI-guided selection with trimmed-mean aggregation under label-flip poisoning; the selection policy itself is not intended as a standalone Byzantine defense. The main practical message is that cloud-edge, privacy-preserving intrusion analytics can improve timeliness through a lightweight scheduling layer without changing the underlying FL participation budget.

</details>


### [72] [SafeHarbor: Hierarchical Memory-Augmented Guardrail for LLM Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05704)
*Zhe Liu,Zonghao Ying,Wenxin Zhang,Quanchen Zou,Deyue Zhang,Dongdong Yang,Xiangzheng Zhang,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeHarbor 是一个针对 LLM 智能体的安全框架，通过上下文感知的防御规则和本地分层记忆系统，在避免过度拒绝（over-refusal）的同时有效防御恶意工具调用攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在工具调用中面临安全风险，现有防御机制存在过度拒绝问题，即在增强安全性时损害了良性任务的实用性，因此需要在不牺牲可用性前提下实现精准边界决策。

Method: 提出 SafeHarbor 框架：(1) 通过增强对抗生成提取上下文感知的防御规则；(2) 设计本地分层记忆系统实现动态规则注入（无训练、即插即用）；(3) 引入基于信息熵的自我进化机制，通过动态节点分裂与合并持续优化记忆结构。

Result: 在 GPT-4o 上良性任务实用率达 63.6%，同时对恶意请求保持超过 93% 的稳健拒绝率，达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: SafeHarbor 有效缓解了安全性与实用性之间的权衡，为 LLM 智能体提供了一种高效、可扩展、无需重新训练的安全防御方案。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of foundation models, Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated increasingly powerful tool-use capabilities. However, this proficiency introduces significant security risks, as malicious actors can manipulate agents into executing tools to generate harmful content. While existing defensive mechanisms are effective, they frequently suffer from the over-refusal problem, where increased safety strictness compromises the agent's utility on benign tasks. To mitigate this trade-off, we propose \textsc{SafeHarbor}, a novel framework designed to establish precise decision boundaries for LLM agents. Unlike static guidelines, \textsc{SafeHarbor} extracts context-aware defense rules through enhanced adversarial generation. We design a local hierarchical memory system for dynamic rule injection, offering a training-free, efficient, and plug-and-play solution. Furthermore, we introduce an information entropy-based self-evolution mechanism that continuously optimizes the memory structure through dynamic node splitting and merging.
