Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textsc{SafeHarbor} achieves state-of-the-art performance on both ambiguous benign tasks and explicit malicious attacks, notably attaining a peak benign utility of 63.6\% on GPT-4o while maintaining a robust refusal rate exceeding 93\% against harmful requests. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/ljj-cyber/SafeHarbor.

</details>


### [73] [$α$-Wasserstein Mechanism for Rényi Pufferfish Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05723)
*Ni Ding,Wenjin Yang,Zijian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出α-Wasserstein机制，通过Hölder不等式将Laplace和Gaussian噪声的尺度参数与Wα度量上界关联，实现Rényi Pufferfish Privacy，并在α=∞时恢复W∞机制。实验表明该方法相比传统W∞方法显著降低噪声功率，且无需δ-近似即可达到精确隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Pufferfish隐私框架主要基于W∞度量，噪声添加机制不够灵活且噪声功率较大。作者希望提出一种更通用的α-Wasserstein框架，利用Rényi散度替代传统ε-差分隐私，在保持严格隐私保证的同时降低噪声注入量，提升数据效用。

Method: 利用Hölder不等式推导Laplace机制中尺度参数与Wα度量上界的关系，建立(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy条件；随后将框架扩展到指数机制；进一步提出适用于Gaussian噪声的Wα机制，覆盖α∈(1,∞)范围。理论分析证明该框架在α=∞时退化为传统W∞机制，并与Rényi差分隐私框架相兼容。

Result: 实验结果表明，α-Wasserstein机制相较于传统W∞方法显著降低噪声功率，其中Gaussian机制优于Laplace机制。该方法在不引入δ-近似松弛的条件下，实现了精确的(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy保证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的α-Wasserstein机制为Pufferfish隐私框架提供了更灵活的噪声校准方案，通过调节α参数在隐私保证与数据效用之间取得更优权衡，且保持严格的隐私保证而非近似松弛。

Abstract: This paper introduces the $α$-Wasserstein mechanism for achieving Rényi Pufferfish Privacy using Laplace and Gaussian noise. By leveraging Hölder's inequality, we demonstrate that the scale parameter of the Laplace mechanism can be calibrated via an upper bound on the $W_α$ metric to satisfy $(α, ε)$-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy for $α\in (1, \infty]$. We show that at the limit $α= \infty$, this framework recovers the established $W_\infty$ mechanism for $ε$-pufferfish privacy. This result is subsequently extended to the exponential mechanism. Furthermore, we propose a $W_α$ mechanism for Gaussian noise for $α\in (1, \infty)$, demonstrating that it generalizes existing results within the Rényi Differential Privacy framework. Experimental evaluations reveal that our $α$-Wasserstein mechanism significantly reduces noise power compared to the conventional $W_\infty$-based approach, with the Gaussian mechanism providing superior utility over the Laplace mechanism. Notably, the mechanisms derived in this work achieve exact $(α, ε)$-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy without requiring additional relaxations, such as $δ$-approximations.

</details>


### [74] [SuperPaymaster: Eliminating Centralized Signer Authority via Asset-Oriented Abstraction to Reconcile Usability and Decentralization in Account Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05774)
*Huifeng Jiao,Nathapon Udomlertsakul*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出资产导向抽象（AOA），通过链上Gas Card替代传统ERC-4337 Paymaster的中心化离线签名机制，在Optimism主网上实现SuperPaymaster，消除审查瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ERC-4337 Paymaster采用过程导向抽象（POA），依赖中心化离线服务器签名赞助请求，形成潜在审查瓶颈，需去中心化改进。

Method: 基于设计科学研究方法，提出资产导向抽象（AOA），将支付能力封装在用户持有的链上Soulbound Token（Gas Card）中，通过确定性策略规则验证赞助有效性，移除离线签名者硬性验证关卡。

Result: Optimism主网测试显示：SuperPaymaster在纯L2执行gas（167,830）中比DEX路由ERC-20基线（328,937）降低49%；与最便宜基线的总计费gas差距主要由bundler定价而非架构差异导致；故障切换模拟表明可绕过非协作中继器。

Conclusion: AOA可在可用性、赞助去中心化和经济效率之间实现更好的平衡，减少三者之间的张力。

Abstract: Most production ERC-4337 Paymasters rely on Process-Oriented Abstraction (POA): a centralized off-chain server signs each sponsorship request and therefore acts as a potential censorship bottleneck. We propose Asset-Oriented Abstraction (AOA), which encapsulates payment capability in a persistent, user-owned on-chain asset -- the ``Gas Card'' -- rather than in an off-chain signing process. Following Design Science Research, we implement SuperPaymaster on Optimism Mainnet. Its sponsorship validity is anchored in on-chain Soulbound Token state and deterministic policy rules, removing the off-chain signer as a hard validity gate. We evaluate gas cost on Optimism Mainnet using single-UserOp ERC-20 transfers ($n{=}50$ per system). Trace-level decomposition isolates an approximately 32k-gas delta as the execution cost of eliminating centralized signing. In pure L2 execution gas, SuperPaymaster (167,830) is lower than both vendor-as-deployed commercial samples, including a 49\% reduction against the DEX-routed ERC-20 baseline (328,937), because it replaces an on-chain liquidation path with an internal balance update. In total billed gas, the remaining gap to the cheapest baseline is explained primarily by bundler pricing rather than paymaster architecture. A failover simulation shows that non-cooperative relayers can be bypassed when an alternative relayer is available. These findings suggest that AOA can reduce the tension among usability, sponsorship decentralization, and economic efficiency.

</details>


### [75] [Stego Battlefield: Evaluating Image Steganography Attacks and Steganalysis Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05789)
*Zhen Sun,Zongmin Zhang,Leyi Sheng,Yule Liu,Yifan Liao,Ke Li,Xinhu Zheng,Jiaheng Wei,Wenyuan Yang,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SADBench是一个系统性的图像隐写安全评估基准框架，包含4项核心任务（攻击评估、防御评估、效率评估、可迁移性评估），通过有害视觉语义和有毒指令模拟恶意攻击，揭示了INN与自编码器稳定性优越、域内检测近乎完美但存在攻击迁移强于防御迁移的不对称性，以及社交媒体上实际威胁持续存在等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏统一、系统的评估框架来衡量隐写术被滥用于绕过内容审核、传播有害语义及向大模型注入恶意指令的安全风险。

Method: 构建了SADBench基准，涵盖4个核心评估任务（隐写攻击能力、隐写分析防御能力、效率、可迁移性），支持图像载荷和文本载荷两种隐写方式，利用有害视觉语义和有毒指令模拟恶意攻击，在多种封面分布下进行评估。

Result: (i) INN和自编码器方法架构稳定性最优；(ii) 域内检测近乎完美且成本低于生成；(iii) 攻击在跨分布迁移时表现出强泛化性，而检测器无法适应，存在显著不对称性；(iv) 社交媒体的实际威胁持续存在，载荷可抵抗轻度压缩或通过模拟训练适应高强度压缩。

Conclusion: SADBench为隐写安全评估提供了系统性、可复现、可扩展的量化框架，推动可衡量的安全驱动的隐写防御技术发展。

Abstract: Image steganography is widely used to protect user privacy and enable covert communication. However, it can also be abused by the adversary as a covert channel to bypass content moderation, disseminate harmful semantics, and even hide malicious instructions in images to elicit dangerous outputs from large models, posing a practical security risk that continues to evolve. To address the lack of a unified and systematic evaluation framework, we propose SADBench, a systematic benchmark that assesses the adversary's ability to inject harmful secrets via steganography and the defender's ability to detect such threats through steganalysis. Crucially, SADBench comprises $4$ core tasks, namely steganography attack capability evaluation, steganalysis defense capability evaluation, efficiency evaluation, and transferability evaluation. It evaluates both image-payload and text-payload steganography across diverse cover distributions, utilizing harmful visual semantics and toxic instructions to simulate malicious attacks. Across a broad set of attacks and detectors, SADBench reveals that (i) INN and autoencoder-based methods demonstrate superior stability compared to other architectures, (ii) in-domain detection is near-perfect and cheaper than generation, (iii) a critical asymmetry exists in transferability where attacks robustly generalize to new distributions while detectors fail to adapt, and (iv) real-world threats persist on social media, where payloads either survive minimal compression or effectively adapt to aggressive compression via simulated training. Overall, SADBench establishes a systematic, reproducible, and extensible framework to quantify risks, paving the way for measurable and security-driven advancements in steganography defense.

</details>


### [76] [LCC-LLM: Leveraging Code-Centric Large Language Models for Malware Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05807)
*Christopher G. Pedraza Pohlenz,Hassan Jalil Hadi,Ali Hassan,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 LCC-LLM，一个以代码为中心的基准数据集和证据驱动的框架，用于恶意软件归因和多任务静态分析。LCCD 数据集包含约 34K PE 样本的多维度逆向工程表示，框架融合 LangGraph 编排的静态分析、七层 RAG 流水线、CoVE 验证和多维质量门控，微调 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B 和 Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B。在 43 种任务类型上平均语义相似度 0.634，真实案例中结构化分析通过率 10/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于 LLM 的恶意软件归因受限于不支持的指标和缺乏代码级别的证据基础，难以准确识别恶意和脆弱代码段。

Method: 构建 LCCD 数据集（~34K PE 样本），包含反编译 C 代码、汇编、CFG/FCG、十六进制数据、PE 元数据、可疑 API 证据等；采用 LangGraph 编排七层 RAG 流水线，集成 CoVE 进行 IoC 验证和多维质量门控；使用课程顺序指令数据通过 QLoRA 微调 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B 和 Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B。

Result: 43 种恶意软件分析任务类型上平均语义相似度 0.634，结构化报告生成、IoC 提取、漏洞评估、恶意软件配置提取和类别检测等任务表现最佳；MalwareBazaar 真实案例中结构化分析通过率 10/10。

Conclusion: 以代码为中心的表示、检索增强和验证引导推理能够提升 LLM 辅助恶意软件归因的可靠性和实际可用性。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly explored for malware analysis; however, current LLM-based malware attribution remains limited by unsupported indicators and insufficient code-level grounding for identifying malicious and vulnerable code segments. To address these limitations, this research introduces LCC-LLM, a code-centric benchmark dataset and evidence-grounded framework for malware attribution and multi-task static malware analysis. The proposed LCCD dataset contains approximately 34K PE samples processed through a large-scale reverse-engineering pipeline and represented using decompiled C code, assembly code, CFG/FCG artifacts, hexadecimal data, PE metadata, suspicious API evidence, and structural features. Beyond dataset construction, LCC-LLM integrates LangGraph-orchestrated static analysis with multi-source cybersecurity knowledge to support evidence-grounded malware reasoning. The framework employs a seven-layer retrieval-augmented generation pipeline, CoVe for IoC validation, and a multi-dimensional quality gate to improve factual reliability and analyst-oriented decision support. Curriculum-ordered instruction data is used to fine-tune DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B and Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B using QLoRA. Evaluation across 43 malware-analysis task types achieves an average semantic similarity of 0.634, with the highest task-level performance in structured report generation, IoC extraction, vulnerability assessment, malware configuration extraction, and malware class detection. In a real-world case study using MalwareBazaar samples, the grounded pipeline achieves a 10/10 structured analysis pass rate, producing CFG/FCG evidence, MITRE ATT&CK mappings, detection guidance, and analyst-ready reports. These results show that code-centric representations, retrieval grounding, and verification-guided reasoning improve the reliability and operational usefulness of LLM-assisted malware attribution.

</details>


### [77] [LeakDojo: Decoding the Leakage Threats of RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05818)
*Maosen Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Boting Lu,Wenyue Li,Xiaoping Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LeakDojo是一个用于系统评估RAG系统数据库泄漏风险的框架。通过对14个LLM、4个数据集和多种RAG系统的基准测试，发现查询生成与对抗指令对泄漏的贡献独立且可乘积近似，更强的指令遵循能力与更高泄漏风险相关，RAG忠实度提升可能增加泄漏风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在增强LLM能力的同时，将其数据库暴露于泄漏攻击风险中。现有研究缺乏对RAG泄漏风险的系统性评估框架。

Method: 提出LeakDojo可配置框架，对6种现有攻击方法在14个LLM、4个数据集和多种RAG系统上进行基准测试和量化分析。

Result: (1)查询生成和对抗指令对泄漏的贡献是独立的，整体泄漏可用两者乘积近似；(2)更强的指令遵循能力与更高泄漏风险正相关；(3)RAG忠实度的改进可能引入更高的泄漏风险。

Conclusion: 这些发现为实践中理解和缓解RAG泄漏提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables large language models (LLMs) to leverage external knowledge, but also exposes valuable RAG databases to leakage attacks. As RAG systems grow more complex and LLMs exhibit stronger instruction-following capabilities, existing studies fall short of systematically assessing RAG leakage risks. We present LeakDojo, a configurable framework for controlled evaluation of RAG leakage. Using LeakDojo, we benchmark six existing attacks across fourteen LLMs, four datasets, and diverse RAG systems. Our study reveals that (1) query generation and adversarial instructions contribute independently to leakage, with overall leakage well approximated by their product; (2) stronger instruction-following capability correlates with higher leakage risk; and (3) improvements in RAG faithfulness can introduce increased leakage risk. These findings provide actionable insights for understanding and mitigating RAG leakage in practice. Our codebase is available at https://github.com/yeasen-z/LeakDojo.

</details>


### [78] [LoopTrap: Termination Poisoning Attacks on LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05846)
*Huiyu Xu,Zhibo Wang,Wenhui Zhang,Ziqi Zhu,Yaopeng Wang,Kui Ren,Chun Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM智能体在执行循环中因恶意提示导致终止判断被扭曲的安全威胁（Termination Poisoning），提出了10种攻击策略，并设计了自动化红队测试框架LoopTrap，通过行为画像和自适应陷阱合成实现平均3.57倍、最高25倍的步骤放大效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体依赖自评估循环来决定任务是否完成，这种机制存在安全隐患——攻击者可通过注入恶意提示来扭曲智能体的终止判断，使其认为任务永远未完成，从而导致无限计算消耗。本文旨在系统定义、刻画并应对这一威胁。

Method: 首先定义了Termination Poisoning威胁并设计了10种代表性攻击策略；然后在8种LLM智能体和60个任务上进行实证研究，发现不同智能体具有独特的行为特征可指导攻击策略选择；最后提出LoopTrap框架，包含行为画像构建（四个脆弱性维度）、自适应陷阱合成（自评分机制选择最优策略和注入点）、以及成功陷阱抽象为可复用技能库与失败尝试的自我反思改进。

Result: 实证研究表明不同LLM智能体存在可迁移的行为模式特征，可用于指导针对未知智能体和任务的有效攻击。LoopTrap在8种主流智能体上实现了平均3.57倍的步骤放大，最高达到25倍。

Conclusion: Termination Poisoning是LLM智能体安全中的一个重要威胁，通过行为特征分析与自适应攻击合成可以自动化地发现和利用这一漏洞，为大规模红队测试提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents solve complex tasks by operating in iterative execution loops, where they repeatedly reason, act, and self-evaluate progress to determine when a task is complete. In this work, we show that while this self-directed loop facilitates autonomy, it also introduces a critical risk: by injecting malicious prompts into the agent's context, an adversary can distort the agent's termination judgment, making it believe the task remains incomplete and leading to unbounded computation.To understand this threat, we define and systematically characterize it as Termination Poisoning and design 10 representative attack strategies. Through a empirical study spanning 8 LLM agents and 60 tasks, we demonstrate that different LLM agents exhibit distinct behavioral signatures that determine which strategies succeed. These transferable patterns can serve as principled guidance for crafting effective attacks against previously unseen agents and tasks, enabling scalable red-teaming beyond manually designed templates. Building on these insights, we introduce LoopTrap, an automated red-teaming framework that synthesizes target-specific malicious prompts by exploiting agent behavioral tendencies. LoopTrap first constructs a behavioral profile of the target agent along four vulnerability dimensions via lightweight probing. It then performs adaptive trap synthesis, routing to the most effective strategy and selecting optimal injections via a self-scoring mechanism. Finally, successful traps are abstracted into a reusable skill library, while failed attempts are refined through self-reflection, ensuring continuous improvement. Extensive evaluation shows that LoopTrap achieves an average of 3.57$\times$ step amplification across 8 mainstream agents, with a peak of 25$\times$.

</details>


### [79] [SkillScope: Toward Fine-Grained Least-Privilege Enforcement for Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05868)
*Jiangrong Wu,Yuhong Nan,Yixi Lin,Huaijin Wang,Yuming Xiao,Shuai Wang,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillScope是一个基于图分析的框架，用于检测和约束LLM Agent Skill中的过度权限行为，在保持任务完成的同时减少88.56%的过度权限动作，并在真实环境中发现了7,039个存在过度权限的Skill。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skill生态系统日益增长，但Skill可能执行超出用户当前任务最小必要范围的高影响动作，违反最小权限原则。现有检测方法无法解决这一任务条件性的问题——同一动作在一个提示下是必要的，在另一个提示下则是过度权限的。

Method: SkillScope采用基于图的分析方法，将指令级过程和代码级操作建模为细粒度动作节点；提取潜在过度权限候选；通过基于重放的分析在图实例化的用户任务下进行验证；并通过控制流权限约束来限制验证后的过度权限动作。

Result: SkillScope在Skill过度权限检测上达到94.53%的F1值；在真实环境中验证了7,039个存在过度权限行为的Skill；在权限约束评估中，减少了88.56%的触发过度权限动作实例，同时保留了合法任务完成。

Conclusion: 最小权限违规在当前的Skill生态系统中普遍存在，SkillScope能够有效检测并约束这些违规行为，为Agent Skill的安全使用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Agent Skills have become a practical way to extend LLM agents by packaging metadata, natural-language instructions, and executable resources into reusable capability bundles. However, this growing Skill ecosystem introduces a new compliance risk: a Skill may perform high-impact actions that exceed the minimum necessary scope of the user's current task, thereby violating least-privilege. Existing skill detection approaches are insufficient for this problem because it is inherently task-conditioned: the same action may be necessary under one user prompt but over-privileged under another. In this paper, we present SkillScope, a framework for fine-grained least-privilege enforcement in Agent Skills. SkillScope adopts a graph-based analysis approach that models instruction-level procedures and code-level operations as fine-grained action nodes. It extracts potential over-privilege candidates, validates them under graph-instantiated user tasks through replay-based analysis, and constrains validated over-privileged actions via control-flow privilege constraining. We evaluate SkillScope through effectiveness experiments and large-scale real-world measurement. SkillScope achieves 94.53% F1 for skill over-privilege detection. In the wild, SkillScope validates 7,039 Skills with over-privileged behaviors, showing that least-privilege violations are prevalent in current Skill ecosystems. In the privilege-constraining evaluation, SkillScope reduces triggered over-privileged action-in-task instances by 88.56% while preserving legitimate task completion.

</details>


### [80] [Toward Space-Based Public Key Systems: Enabling Secure Space Communications through In-Orbit Trust Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05948)
*Rehana Yasmin,Paulo Esteves-Verissimo,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将公钥基础设施（PKI）从地面迁移至太空的两种架构方案，以解决传统地面PKI在动态空间环境中的延迟和运营瓶颈问题，实现航天资产的高效认证与跨实体协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: New Space时代近地轨道卫星数量激增，需要安全、近实时的协调，但传统依赖地面的PKI存在延迟高、运营瓶颈大、可扩展性和可用性受限等问题，无法满足下一代空间任务需求。

Method: 提出两种部署方案：(1) 空间-地面集成PKI，在轨部署验证权威机构；(2) 完全自主的空间PKI，实现在轨证书签发与验证。并从可扩展性、可用性、安全性、成本和运营复杂度等维度分析权衡，提供基线延迟分析。

Result: 提出了两种面向多运营商环境的空间PKI架构设计，给出了部署权衡分析，并提供了在轨信任管理的性能延迟分析。

Conclusion: 基于空间的PKI架构能够减少对地面站的依赖，提升跨实体互操作性和协作能力，是解决New Space时代航天资产认证问题的可行方向。

Abstract: The New Space era has led to a rapid increase in satellites operated by independent entities in near-Earth orbit. This shift enables richer space services but also requires secure, near-real-time coordination, making efficient authentication of space assets critical for next-generation missions. Traditional ground-dependent Public Key Infrastructure (PKI) suffers from latency and operational bottlenecks that limit scalability and availability in dynamic space environments. This paper proposes architectural designs for space-based PKI that shift certificate management and validation from ground infrastructure into space, reducing reliance on ground stations while enabling interoperability and cross-entity collaboration. Two deployment schemes are introduced: a space-ground integrated PKI with in-orbit validation authorities, and a fully autonomous space-based PKI with in-space issuance and validation. We analyze deployment trade-offs in scalability, availability, security, cost, and operational complexity in multi-operator environments. A baseline latency analysis is provided to illustrate performance implications of in-orbit trust management.

</details>


### [81] [Heimdallr: Characterizing and Detecting LLM-Induced Security Risks in GitHub CI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05969)
*Bonan Ruan,Yeqi Fu,Chuqi Zhang,Jiahao Liu,Jun Zeng,Zhenkai Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了GitHub CI工作流中由LLM引入的安全风险，提出了风险分类体系，设计了混合分析框架Heimdallr（基于LLM-工作流属性图L-WPG），在300个手工标注的工作流上实现了高精度检测，并已负责任地披露了802个漏洞实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub CI工作流越来越多地集成LLM来自动化代码审查、问题分类、内容生成和仓库维护，这带来了新的攻击面：外部可控的工作流输入可以塑造LLM的提示和输出，进而影响安全决策、仓库状态或特权执行。尽管LLM安全和CI安全各自已被广泛研究，但它们的交叉领域仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 论文设计了一个混合分析框架Heimdallr，将工作流标准化为LLM-工作流属性图（L-WPG），结合可触发分析、LLM辅助的数据流摘要和确定性传播，来合成具体的威胁向量发现。

Result: 在300个手工标注的工作流上，Heimdallr在LLM节点识别（F1=0.994）、可触发分类（99.8%）和威胁向量检测（微平均F1=0.917）上均达到了高精度。已负责任地披露了802个漏洞实例，覆盖759个仓库，获得71个确认。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了LLM集成到CI工作流中带来的新型安全风险，并提供了有效的检测方法。该工作属于对LLM安全与CI安全交叉领域的首次系统研究。

Abstract: GitHub Continuous Integration (CI) workflows increasingly integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate review, triage, content generation, and repository maintenance. This creates a new attack surface: externally controllable workflow inputs can shape LLM prompts and outputs, which may in turn affect security decisions, repository state, or privileged execution. Although LLM security and CI security have each been studied extensively, their intersection remains underexplored. In this paper, we present the first study of LLM-induced security risks in GitHub CI workflows. We characterize the problem along the full execution chain and develop a taxonomy of high-level risk classes and concrete threat vectors. To detect such risks in practice, we design Heimdallr, a hybrid analysis framework that normalizes workflows into an LLM-Workflow Property Graph (L-WPG) and combines triggerability analysis, LLM-assisted dataflow summarization, and deterministic propagation to synthesize concrete threat-vector findings. Evaluated on 300 manually annotated unique workflows, Heimdallr achieves high accuracy on LLM-node identification (F1~=~0.994), triggerability classification (99.8%), and threat-vector detection (micro-average F1~=~0.917). As part of an ongoing detection and disclosure effort, we have so far responsibly disclosed 802 vulnerable workflow instances across 759 repositories and received 71 acknowledgments.

</details>


### [82] [PragLocker: Protecting Agent Intellectual Property in Untrusted Deployments via Non-Portable Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05974)
*Qinfeng Li,Yuntai Bao,Jianghui Hu,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Huifeng Zhu,Yier Jin,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PragLocker 提出了一种针对 LLM Agent 提示词的保护方案，通过语义锚定和噪声注入构建功能保留的混淆提示，使其仅能在目标 LLM 上有效工作，从而防止提示词被窃取和跨模型复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 的提示词是宝贵的知识产权，但在不可信部署环境中容易被敌手复制并用于其他专有 LLM，造成经济损失。现有方法无法满足主动性、运行时保护、可用性和不可移植性这四个关键要求。

Method: PragLocker 通过两个步骤构建功能保留的混淆提示：首先用代码符号锚定语义，然后利用目标模型的反馈注入噪声，生成的提示仅能在目标 LLM 上正常工作。

Result: 在多个 Agent 系统、数据集和基础 LLM 上的实验表明，PragLocker 显著降低了跨 LLM 的可移植性，同时保持了目标任务性能，并且对自适应攻击者具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PragLocker 有效解决了 LLM Agent 提示词保护中的关键挑战，在防止提示词被盗用复用的同时，保证了目标模型上的可用性和安全性。

Abstract: LLM agents rely on prompts to implement task-specific capabilities based on foundation LLMs, making agent prompts valuable intellectual property. However, in untrusted deployments, adversaries can copy and reuse these prompts with other proprietary LLMs, causing economic losses. To protect these prompts, we identify four key challenges: proactivity, runtime protection, usability, and non-portability that existing approaches fail to address. We present PragLocker, a prompt protection scheme that satisfies these requirements. PragLocker constructs function-preserving obfuscated prompts by anchoring semantics with code symbols and then using target-model feedback to inject noise, yielding prompts that only work on the target LLM. Experiments across multiple agent systems, datasets, and foundation LLMs show that PragLocker substantially reduces cross-LLM portability, maintains target performance, and remains robust against adaptive attackers.

</details>


### [83] [Safety Anchor: Defending Harmful Fine-tuning via Geometric Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05995)
*Guoxin Lu,Letian Sha,Qing Wang,Peijie Sun,Hao Zhou,Hua Dai,Fu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型的有害微调攻击，本文提出安全瓶颈正则化（SBR）方法，通过将有害查询的最终隐藏状态锚定到安全对齐模型的隐藏状态，在解嵌入层这一几何瓶颈处实施防御，仅需单个安全锚点即可将有害分数降至10以下，同时保持良性任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法（约束参数、梯度或内部表示）在高维参数空间的冗余性下，无法抵御持续的有害微调攻击，攻击者可以利用与防御约束正交的优化轨迹绕过防御。

Method: 提出安全瓶颈正则化（SBR），将防御焦点从冗余的参数空间转移到解嵌入层（几何瓶颈），通过将有害查询的最终隐藏状态锚定到安全对齐模型的对应隐藏状态，使模型在持续有害微调下仍能生成安全响应。

Result: 仅需单个安全锚点即可将有害分数降至10以下，同时在良性下游任务上保持竞争性能。

Conclusion: SBR通过聚焦解嵌入层这一几何瓶颈，有效解决了高维参数空间冗余导致的防御失效问题，是对抗有害微调的有效且高效的方法。

Abstract: The safety alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) remains vulnerable to Harmful Fine-tuning (HFT). While existing defenses impose constraints on parameters, gradients, or internal representations, we observe that they can be effectively circumvented under persistent HFT. Our analysis traces this failure to the inherent redundancy of the high-dimensional parameter space: attackers exploit optimization trajectories that are orthogonal to defense constraints to restore harmful capabilities while deceptively adhering to safety restrictions. To address this, we propose Safety Bottleneck Regularization (SBR). SBR shifts the defensive focus from the redundant parameter space to the unembedding layer, which serves as a geometric bottleneck. By anchoring the final hidden states of harmful queries to those of the safety-aligned model, SBR enables the model to maintain safe responses even under persistent HFT. Extensive experiments confirm SBR's effectiveness, demonstrating that utilizing just a single safety anchor is sufficient to reduce the Harmful Score to $<$10 while preserving competitive performance on benign downstream tasks.

</details>


### [84] [Secure Seed-Based Multi-bit Watermarking for Diffusion Models from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06153)
*Enoal Gesny,Eva Giboulot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式图像水印技术缺乏严格理论分析的问题，提出将模型依赖部分与决策机制解耦，构建基于安全性、鲁棒性和保真度的形式化评估框架，并设计了一种新的种子基水印方法SSB，可从理论上刻画三者的权衡曲面，无需依赖特定生成模型进行经验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式图像水印方法（尤其是种子基嵌入）的评估高度依赖特定生成模型架构，缺乏严格的安全定义和理论分析，导致无法对方法性能（特别是安全性）得出明确结论。

Method: 通过解耦水印系统中模型依赖部分与决策机制，引入基于安全性、鲁棒性和保真度的形式化评估框架，利用特征曲面刻画三者间的权衡关系，并提出SSB方法对该曲面上的任意工作点进行参数化实现。

Result: SSB方法能够泛化现有种子基方法，可在特征曲面上达到任意安全-鲁棒-保真度组合，且框架具有模型无关性，无需昂贵的经验评估即可获得理论保证。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计具有理论保证的现代水印系统开辟了新路径，摆脱了对经验评估的依赖，实现了严格的理论分析与性能比较。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of generative image models has led to the development of specialized watermarking techniques, particularly in-generation methods such as seed-based embedding. However, current evaluations in this area remain largely empirical, making them heavily reliant on the specific model architectures used for generation and inversion. This prevents any clear conclusion on the performance of any method, especially regarding security, for which a rigorous definition is lacking. Against this approach, we argue that the effectiveness of a watermarking scheme should be established purely through a thorough theoretical analysis. This is enabled by decoupling the model-dependent part from the actual decision mechanism of the watermarking system. Using this decoupling, we introduce a formal evaluation framework based on security, robustness, and fidelity. This allows precise comparisons between watermarking systems through a characteristic surface representing the trade-off between these three quantities, independent of any generative model. Based on this framework, we propose SSB, a novel watermarking method that generalizes previous seed-based methods by allowing to reach any security-robustness-fidelity regime on its characteristic surface. This work opens the door to the design of modern watermarking systems with theoretical guarantees that do not necessitate any costly empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [85] [Stateful Agent Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06158)
*Zhengchunmin Dai,Jiaxiong Tang,Liantao Wu,Peng Sun,Honglong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨会话、持久化的有状态智能体后门攻击，将攻击生命周期从单次会话扩展到多次会话，通过形式化建模为Mealy机并分解为独立转换，实现了80%-95%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对大语言模型智能体的后门攻击是无状态的，只能在单次会话内执行固定行为，无法在权限隔离环境下跨会话持续运行。本文旨在突破这一限制，实现跨会话的持久化后门攻击。

Method: 将后门攻击形式化为Mealy机模型，提出分解框架实现独立逐转换的数据构建；通过持久化组件维护状态，实现一次触发注入后的跨会话自主增量执行；并实例化主要攻击及两种可扩展变体。

Result: 主要实例化在四个模型上达到80%-95%的攻击成功率，逐转换分析验证了分解的有效性；可扩展变体在不同拓扑结构和持久化组件下表现一致有效。

Conclusion: 本文提出的有状态后门攻击成功将攻击生命周期从单会话扩展到多会话，形式化建模和分解框架为后续研究提供了理论基础，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Existing backdoor attacks on Large Language Model-based agents remain stateless, executing fixed behaviors confined to a single session. We propose a stateful agent backdoor that extends the attack lifecycle across multiple sessions under permission isolation. The attack maintains state through persistent components, enabling autonomous, incremental execution across sessions following a one-time trigger injection. Formally, we model the attack as a Mealy machine and derive a decomposition framework that enables independent per-transition data construction. We instantiate this framework with a primary attack and two extensibility variants. The primary instantiation achieves an attack success rate of 80\%--95\% across four models, with per-transition analysis demonstrating the effectiveness of the decomposition. Extensibility variants with alternative topologies and persistent components demonstrate consistent effectiveness. Code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/stateful_agent_backdoor-E89F.

</details>


### [86] [ClawGuard: Out-of-Band Detection of LLM Agent Workflow Hijacking via EM Side Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06205)
*Leo Linqian Gan,Jeffery Wu,Longyuan Ge,Lanqing Yang,Yonghao Song,Jingkai Zhang,Haojia Jin,Weiyi Wang,Guangtao Xue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ClawGuard提出了一种基于电磁辐射的无源侧信道监控方法，通过分析LLM智能体执行任务时产生的硬件级电磁信号，实现对工作流劫持攻击的物理层检测，在7.82TB射频数据集上达到0.9945的AUC和100%的真正率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于主机内部遥测（如审计日志）的防御方案在操作系统被攻陷时可能被伪造，亟需一种无法被软件篡改的、独立于主机的物理层监控手段来检测LLM智能体的工作流劫持攻击。

Method: 利用软件定义无线电（SDR）从外部无源捕获LLM智能体执行不同技能时产生的电磁辐射信号，通过漂移感知流水线提取320维特征，将RF流转化为可执行的物理证据，从而识别工具和技能调用的异常。

Result: 在7.82TB的RF语料库上，ClawGuard的AUC达到0.9945，攻击检测真正率100%，假正率仅1.16%。

Conclusion: 无源电磁感知是一种实用且不可伪造的物理层校验手段，能够有效抵御主机软件被攻陷场景下的LLM智能体工作流劫持攻击。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents face a critical security risk known as workflow hijacking, where attackers subtly alter tool and skill invocations. Existing defenses rely on host-internal telemetry (such as audit logs), which can be forged if the host OS is compromised. To solve this, we introduce ClawGuard, a passive, out-of-band monitor that audits LLM-agent workflows using electromagnetic (EM) emanations. Because distinct agent skills create unique hardware usage patterns (computation, DRAM, network blocking), they emit measurable, macroscopic EM envelopes. External software-defined radios (SDRs) capture these physical signals. Using a drift-aware pipeline with 320-dimensional features, ClawGuard converts RF streams into physical evidence. Evaluated on a 7.82TB RF corpus, ClawGuard achieved an AUC of 0.9945, detecting attacks with a 100% true-positive rate and a 1.16% false-positive rate. This proves passive EM sensing is a practical, forge-resistant physical check against compromised host software.

</details>


### [87] [Profiling for Pennies: Unveiling the Privacy Iceberg of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06232)
*Jiahao Chen,Qi Zhang,Ruixiao Lin,Chunyi Zhou,Tianyu Du,Qingming Li,Tong Zhang,Junhao Li,Yuwen Pu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM平台存在隐私泄露的严重问题：平台未能从技术或政策层面解决公众隐私担忧。作者提出PrivacyIceberg框架，将隐私风险分为三个层级，并开发IcebergExplorer工具，仅用最小PII作为种子，10分钟内以低于3美元成本重建高精度个人档案（事实准确率超90%）。同时识别了六项根本原因并提出多方对策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要从LLM训练管线角度研究隐私（关注通过记忆泄露PII），缺乏从人类中心视角审视现实世界中的隐私感知与实践。为填补这一空白，本文从人类意识和LLM集成平台实践角度调查现实中的隐私感知，发现平台未能解决公众隐私担忧。

Method: 1) 通过实证研究调查现实世界中人类隐私感知与LLM平台实践之间的失调。2) 提出PrivacyIceberg框架，将基于LLM利用的隐私风险分为三层：显式搜索、上下文推断、深度聚合。3) 开发IcebergExplorer审计工具，以最小PII为搜索种子重建个人档案。4) 识别隐私泄露的六项根本原因并提出多利益相关方对策。

Result: IcebergExplorer在真实场景中实现超过90%的事实准确性，用时不到10分钟，成本低于3美元。识别出六项导致隐私泄露的根本原因。

Conclusion: LLM集成平台存在严重的隐私风险与实践脱节问题，需要LLM供应商、个人和数据发布者等多方采取对策应对。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized how information are collected, aggregated, and reasoned. However, this enables a novel and accessible vector of privacy intrusion: the automated and in-depth personal profiling; this engenders a chilling effect of "peepers everywhere". Existing research primarily unfolds from the training pipeline of LLM, emphasizing the exposure of Personally Identifiable Information (PII) through memorization, while privacy studies from a human-centric perspective remain underexplored. To fill this void, we empirically investigate privacy perception in the real world through the lens of human awareness and the practices of LLM-integrated platforms, revealing a significant dissonance: platforms fail to technically or policy-wise address public privacy concerns. To facilitate a systematic and quantifiable study of privacy risk, we propose the PrivacyIceberg, which categorizes real-world human privacy risks into three tiers: explicitly searched, contextually inferred, and deeply aggregated, based on the sophistication of LLM exploitation. We developed IcebergExplorer to audit privacy exposure, utilizing minimal PII as a search seed to reconstruct high-fidelity profiles, achieving over 90% factual accuracy within 10 minutes at a cost under $3, for real-world scenarios. Additionally, we identify six root causes contributing to such privacy disclosures and propose multi-stakeholder countermeasures for LLM vendors, individuals, and data publishers.

</details>


### [88] [Fine-Tuning Small Language Models for Solution-Oriented Windows Event Log Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06330)
*Siraaj Akhtar,Saad Khan,Simon Parkinson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究微调后的小语言模型（SLM）在事件日志分析中能否替代大语言模型（LLM）。作者创建了包含修复操作的大规模合成Windows事件日志数据集，使用LoRA技术微调多个SLM和LLM。结果表明，微调后的SLM在问题识别和修复建议方面持续优于LLM，且计算资源需求更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在事件日志分析中虽有潜力，但存在计算需求高、依赖云基础设施、安全隐患等实际部署问题；且现有方法仅关注问题识别，无法提供可操作的修复方案。SLM作为轻量级替代方案，可针对特定任务微调并本地部署，但尚不清楚其能否在实际事件日志分析中替代LLM并同时生成解决方案。

Method: 1) 利用高性能LLM创建大规模合成Windows事件日志数据集，包含修复操作；2) 使用LoRA参数高效微调技术对多个SLM和LLM进行微调；3) 通过专家评估比较模型性能。

Result: 数据集准确反映真实场景；微调后的SLM在问题识别和提供相关修复建议方面持续优于LLM，且计算资源需求更低。

Conclusion: 针对特定任务微调的SLM可作为事件日志分析中LLM的实用替代方案，既能识别问题也能生成解决方案，同时大幅降低计算资源需求。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for event log analysis, but their high computational requirements, reliance on cloud infrastructure, and security concerns limit practical deployment. In addition, most existing approaches focus only on the identification of the problem and do not provide actionable remediation. Small language models (SLMs) present a light-weight alternative that can be fine-tuned for a specific purpose and hosted locally. This paper investigates whether SLMs, when fine-tuned for a specific task, can serve as a practical alternative for event log analysis while also generating solutions. We first create a large-scale synthetic Windows event log dataset that contains remediation actions using a high-performing LLM. We then fine-tune multiple SLMs and LLMs using the LoRA parameter-efficient fine-tuning technique and evaluate their performance by comparing with expert assessment. The results show that the dataset accurately reflects real-world scenarios and that fine-tuned SLMs consistently outperform LLMs in identifying issues and providing relevant remediation, while requiring fewer computational resources.

</details>


### [89] [Constraining Host-Level Abuse in Self-Hosted Computer-Use Agents via TEE-Backed Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06393)
*Di Lu,Bo Zhang,Xiyuan Li,Yongzhi Liao,Xuewen Dong,Yulong Shen,Zhiquan Liu,Jianfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自托管计算机智能体（SHCUA）的操作中心风险约束模型，通过云原生TEE支撑的可信操作平面来保护关键操作，并在OpenClaw+Intel TDX上实现，能够阻止不安全操作、保留正常功能并提供可审计证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自托管计算机智能体（如OpenClaw）具备自然语言交互和宿主资源直接访问能力，面临来自恶意消息、间接提示注入、不安全技能及宿主控制路径篡改等多方面的滥用风险，而这些风险无法仅通过临时阻断规则解决，因为操作的安全关键性取决于动作类型、目标对象、执行上下文和潜在效果等多个因素。

Method: 提出操作中心的风险约束模型：将普通功能保留在受限的REE路径上，把安全关键的分类、授权、绑定、证据生成及执行控制决策保护在云原生TEE支撑的可信操作平面内；在OpenClaw上以Intel TDX为主要可信后端进行实例化，远程终端侧的可信组件在本地约束执行前验证经TDX审计的命令。

Result: 该设计能够在执行前阻断不安全或策略不允许的操作，保持允许工作负载的普通功能正常运行，并提供可审计的证据，但存在与部署相关的开销。

Conclusion: 操作中心的风险约束模型是应对SHCUA宿主级滥用风险的有效方案，平衡了安全性与功能性，TEE与远程可信组件的结合为安全关键操作提供了可审计的保障机制。

Abstract: Self-hosted computer-use agents (SHCUAs), such as OpenClaw, combine natural-language interaction with direct access to host-side resources, including browsers, files, scripts, system commands, and external communication channels. While useful for automating real tasks, this capability also creates a host-level abuse surface: a legitimately deployed agent may be steered toward unsafe operations through malicious messages, indirect prompt injection, unsafe skills, or tampering along the host-side control path. We argue that such risks cannot be addressed by ad hoc blocking rules alone, because the security criticality of an operation depends jointly on its action type, target object, execution context, and potential effect.
  This paper presents an operation-centric model for risk-based confinement of SHCUA operations. The proposed design keeps ordinary functionality on the constrained REE path, while protecting security-critical classification, authorization, binding, evidence generation, and selected execution-control decisions inside a cloud-native TEE-backed trusted operation plane. We instantiate the architecture on OpenClaw using Intel TDX as the primary trusted backend, with remote terminal-side trusted components verifying TDX-audited commands before constrained local execution. The evaluation shows that the design can block unsafe or policy-disallowed operations before execution, preserve ordinary functionality for allowed workloads, and provide auditable evidence with deployment-dependent overhead.

</details>


### [90] [Pop Quiz Attack: Black-box Membership Inference Attacks Against Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06423)
*Zeyuan Chen,Yihan Ma,Xinyue Shen,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PopQuiz Attack，一种将训练数据转化为多项选择题进行黑盒成员推理攻击的方法，在6个主流LLM和4个数据集上平均ROC-AUC达0.873，比现有方法提升20.6%，并评估了多种防御措施的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可能记忆并泄露训练数据，带来严重的隐私风险。现有成员推理攻击方法效果有限，需要更有效的攻击方法来评估和揭示LLM的隐私漏洞。

Method: 将目标数据转化为多项选择题（quiz-style），通过黑盒方式查询模型对这些问题的回答，根据回答的正确性和模式推断该数据是否属于训练集（成员推理攻击）。

Result: 在6个LLM（GPT-3.5, GPT-4o, LLaMA2-7b, LLaMA2-13b, Mistral-7b, Vicuna-7b）和4个数据集上，平均ROC-AUC达到0.873，比现有SOTA方法提升20.6%。分析了查询复杂度、数据类型、数据结构和训练设置等因素的影响。

Conclusion: 现代LLM存在显著的隐私漏洞。指令过滤、基于过滤和差分隐私等防御措施虽能降低攻击性能，但无法完全消除风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong performance across many applications, but their ability to memorize and potentially reveal training data raises serious privacy concerns. We introduce the PopQuiz Attack, a black-box membership inference attack that tests whether a model can recall specific training examples. The core idea is to turn target data into quiz-style multiple-choice questions and infer membership from the model's answers. Across six widely used LLMs (GPT-3.5, GPT-4o, LLaMA2-7b, LLaMA2-13b, Mistral-7b, and Vicuna-7b) and four datasets, our method achieves an average ROC-AUC of 0.873 and outperforms existing approaches by 20.6%. We further analyze factors affecting attack success, including query complexity, data type, data structure, and training settings. We also evaluate instruction-based, filter-based, and differential privacy-based defenses, which reduce performance but do not eliminate the risk. Our results highlight persistent privacy vulnerabilities in modern LLMs.

</details>


### [91] [Privacy by Postprocessing the Discrete Laplace Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06502)
*Quentin Hillebrand,Jacob Imola,Rasmus Pagh,Sia Sejer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明经典离散拉普拉斯机制（几何机制）经过后处理可以：(1) 对任意次指数函数f生成简单无偏估计量，是Calmon等人(FORC '25)拉普拉斯机制无偏结果在离散多变量场景的延伸；(2) 输出与拉普拉斯机制或阶梯机制相同分布。因此离散拉普拉斯机制在数据离散（或可控ℓ1敏感度离散化）时应优先于拉普拉斯和阶梯机制。文章给出估计量方差界，虽然最坏情况指数时间复杂度，但对常见结构化函数可线性/多项式时间计算，并通过轮廓估计、熵估计及联邦分析等用例展示其性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散拉普拉斯机制虽经典但未充分发掘其后处理能力，尤其是在无偏估计方面的潜力。现有Calmon等人的无偏结果仅针对连续拉普拉斯机制，缺乏离散场景的对应方案。同时，实际应用中无偏性对准确性至关重要（如联邦学习场景），需要一种既能满足差分隐私又能提供无偏估计的通用机制。

Method: (1) 提出离散拉普拉斯机制的后处理方法，对任意次指数函数f生成无偏估计量，通过巧妙设计后处理步骤消除原始机制的偏差；(2) 证明通过后处理可使离散拉普拉斯机制输出与连续拉普拉斯机制或阶梯机制相同分布；(3) 对估计量的方差进行理论分析，并与直接评估f在有噪输出上的均方误差进行比较；(4) 针对常见结构化函数（如轮廓估计、熵估计）设计线性/多项式时间算法，避免最坏情况指数复杂度。

Result: 理论结果表明离散拉普拉斯机制后处理可实现无偏估计，且方差有界；实证结果通过轮廓估计、熵估计和分布式/联邦数据分析等用例展示了方法的有效性，特别是在无偏性对精度关键的场景中表现优异。

Conclusion: 离散拉普拉斯机制是一种多功能机制，在数据为离散或可离散化且能控制ℓ1敏感度时，应优先于拉普拉斯机制和阶梯机制使用。其无偏估计能力在联邦学习等分布式场景中尤为重要。

Abstract: We show that an "old dog", the classical discrete Laplace (aka.~geometric) mechanism, can "perform new tricks":
  1. It can be post-processed to yield a simple, unbiased estimator of any subexponential function $f$ of the original data, giving a simple, discrete, multivariate version of the recent unbiasing result for the Laplace mechanism by Calmon et al. (FORC '25).
  2. It can be post-processed to output the same distribution as the Laplace mechanism or the Staircase mechanism with identical privacy parameters.
  Thus, the discrete Laplace mechanism is a versatile mechanism that should be preferred over the Laplace and Staircase mechanisms whenever the data is discrete (or can be made discrete while controlling $\ell_1$-sensitivity).
  We show bounds on the variance of our estimator, compared to the mean square error of the biased estimator that simply evaluates the $f$ on the output of the mechanism. Though our unbiased estimator has exponential running time for worst-case functions, we show that it can often be computed in linear or polynomial time for some common functions exhibiting structure. We showcase the properties of our methods empirically with several use cases including profile and entropy estimation, as well as distributed/federated data analysis applications in which unbiasedness is key to accuracy.

</details>


### [92] [On the Security of Research Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06508)
*Nanda Rani,Christian Rossow*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了顶级安全会议中的509个研究工件，发现41.60%的常见发现存在潜在安全风险。作者提出了上下文感知安全评估分类法，并开发了SAFE框架（自主分析工具报告发现的安全风险），该框架在区分安全与非安全风险上达到84.80%的准确率和84.63%的F1分数，强调了工件评估中引入安全考量的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的工件评估（AE）主要关注工件是否按预期工作及结果是否可复现，但忽视了工件中可能存在的安全风险。由于这些工件被公开发布和重用，可能无意中引入攻击向量，因此需要系统地评估和缓解这些安全风险。

Method: 收集了509个来自顶级安全会议的研究工件，通过静态分析识别不安全代码模式；提出上下文感知安全评估分类法进行结构化风险分析；开发了SAFE框架，通过考虑代码语义、执行上下文和实际可利用性来自动区分安全与非安全风险。

Result: 41.60%的常见发现存在实际使用中的安全风险；SAFE框架在区分安全与非安全风险时达到84.80%的准确率和84.63%的F1分数。

Conclusion: 安全性应成为工件评估中的重要考量维度，以促进安全且负责任的研究共享。SAFE作为自主化安全分析框架的第一步展示了可行性。

Abstract: Research artifacts are widely shared to support reproducibility, and artifact evaluation (AE) has become common at many leading conferences. However, AE mainly checks whether artifacts work as claimed and can be reproduced. It largely overlooks potential security risks. Since these artifacts are publicly released and reused, they may unintentionally create opportunities for misuse and raise concerns about safe and responsible sharing. We study 509 research artifacts from top-tier security venues and find that many contain insecure code patterns that may introduce potential attack vectors. We propose a taxonomy for context-aware security assessment to enable structured analysis of such risks. We perform static analysis and examine the resulting findings, filtering false positives and identifying real security risks. Our analysis shows that 41.60% of the prevalent findings may pose security concerns under practical usage. To support scalable analysis, we introduce SAFE (Security-Aware Framework for Artifact Evaluation), a first step toward an autonomous framework that analyzes tool-reported findings by considering code semantics, execution context, and practical exploitability. SAFE achieves 84.80% accuracy and 84.63% F1-score in distinguishing security and non-security risks. Overall, our results show that security is also important in AE for promoting safe and responsible research sharing. The source code is available at: https://github.com/nanda-rani/SAFE

</details>


### [93] [FedAttr: Towards Privacy-preserving Client-Level Attribution in Federated LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06596)
*Su Zhang,Junfeng Guo,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FedAttr，一种用于联邦学习中客户端级别水印归属的协议，通过配对子集差分机制在保护安全聚合隐私的同时，识别哪些客户端使用了水印数据训练模型，实现了100% TPR和0% FPR，并显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水印放射性检测方法在集中式LLM微调中已证明有效，但在联邦学习场景下，由于安全聚合机制隐藏了各客户端的更新，导致无法识别哪个客户端使用了受水印保护的文档进行训练，因此需要一种既能保护隐私又能实现客户端级别归属的方法。

Method: FedAttr包含三个步骤：(i) 通过对两次安全聚合查询做差分来估计每个客户端的更新；(ii) 通过差分评分方法用水印检测器对估计值进行评分；(iii) 使用Stouffer方法跨轮次组合分数。通过配对子集差分机制实现归属，同时保持安全聚合的隐私保证。

Result: 理论结果表明FedAttr能产生每个客户端更新的无偏估计，且每轮互信息泄漏为O(d*/N)。实验达到100% TPR和0% FPR，TPR或FPR上优于所有基线至少44.4%或19.1%，且仅增加6.3%的训练开销。消融实验证实对协议参数和配置具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedAttr成功解决了联邦学习场景下水印归属的隐私-可溯性矛盾，在保证安全聚合隐私的前提下实现了高效的客户端级别归属，为联邦学习中的数据所有权保护提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Watermark radioactivity testing type of methods can detect whether a model was trained on watermarked documents, and have become key tools for protecting data ownership in the fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Existing works have proved their effectiveness in centralized LLM fine-tuning. However, this type of method faces several challenges and remains underexplored in federated learning (FL), a widely-applied paradigm for fine-tuning LLMs collaboratively on private data across different users. FL mainly ensures privacy through secure aggregation (SA), which allows the server to aggregate updates while keeping clients' updates private. This mechanism preserves privacy but makes it difficult to identify which client trained on watermarked documents. In this work, we propose FedAttr, a new client-level attribution protocol for FL. FedAttr identifies which clients trained on watermarked data via a paired-subset-difference mechanism, while preserving the privacy guarantees of SA and FL performance. FedAttr proceeds in three steps: (i) estimate each client's update by differencing two SA queries, (ii) score the estimate with the watermark detector via differential scoring, and (iii) combine scores across rounds via Stouffer method. We theoretically show that FedAttr produces an unbiased estimator of each client's update with bounded mutual information leakage (i.e., $O(d^*/N)$ per-round update). Moreover, FedAttr empirically achieves 100% TPR and 0% FPR, outperforming all baselines by at least 44.4% in TPR or 19.1% in FPR, with only 6.3% overhead relative to FL training time. Ablation studies confirm that FedAttr is robust to protocol parameters and configurations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
*Alex Oesterling,Donghao Ren,Yannick Assogba,Dominik Moritz,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Leon Gatys,Fred Hohman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可解释的注释者策略模型（APMs），能从标注行为中学习注释者内在的安全策略，无需额外标注成本即可揭示标注分歧的来源（操作失误、策略模糊、价值多元），并验证了模型在准确性和迁移性上的有效性，支持更精准、透明和包容的安全策略设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注分歧在AI安全策略标注中普遍存在，但其来源（操作失误、策略模糊、价值多元）难以区分。直接询问标注者推理过程成本高且不可靠，亟需一种无需额外标注即可揭示标注分歧来源的方法。

Method: 提出注释者策略模型（APMs），这是一种可解释模型，仅从标注行为中学习注释者内部安全策略。通过预测反事实编辑响应、恢复已知策略差异等方式验证模型有效性，并在LLM和人类标注上展示应用。

Result: APM能够以>80%的准确率建模注释者安全策略，忠实预测反事实编辑响应，并在受控设置中恢复已知策略差异。在LLM和人类标注上，成功揭示了策略模糊性和价值多元性。

Conclusion: APMs使得标注分歧来源可区分，支持更有针对性的质量管控、策略澄清和多元价值融入，推动更透明和包容的安全策略设计。

Abstract: Safety policies define what constitutes safe and unsafe AI outputs, guiding data annotation and model development. However, annotation disagreement is pervasive and can stem from multiple sources such as operational failures (annotators misunderstand or misexecute the task), policy ambiguity (policy wording leaves room for interpretation), or value pluralism (different annotators hold different perspectives on safety). Distinguishing these sources matters. For example, operational failures call for quality control, ambiguity calls for policy clarification, and pluralism calls for deliberation about incorporating diverse perspectives. Yet understanding why annotators disagree is difficult. Directly asking annotators for their reasoning is costly, substantially increasing annotation burden, and can be unreliable for both human and LLM annotators as self-reported reasoning often fails to reflect actual decision processes.
  We introduce Annotator Policy Models (APMs), interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, making annotator reasoning visible and comparable without additional annotation effort. We validate that APMs accurately model annotator safety policy (>80% accuracy), faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Applying APMs to LLM and human annotations, we demonstrate two core applications: (1) surfacing policy ambiguity by revealing how annotators interpret safety instructions differently, and (2) surfacing value pluralism by uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups. Together, these capabilities support more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design.

</details>


### [95] [ZAYA1-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
*Robert Washbourne,Rishi Iyer,Tomas Figliolia,Henry Zheng,Ryan Lorig-Roach,Sungyeon Yang,Pritish Yuvraj,Quentin Anthony,Yury Tokpanov,Xiao Yang,Ganesh Nanduru,Stephen Ebert,Praneeth Medepalli,Skyler Szot,Srivatsan Rajagopal,Alex Ong,Bhavana Mehta,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZAYA1-8B是一个基于MoE++架构的推理聚焦混合专家模型，仅700M活跃参数（8B总参数），在AMD全栈平台上训练。它在数学和编程基准上匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528，并通过四阶段RL级联和Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，在AIME'25上达到91.9%，缩小了与更大模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限条件下（<1B活跃参数）构建高性能推理模型，挑战"更大即更好"的范式，并通过全AMD基础设施验证硬件-软件协同设计的可行性。

Method: 采用MoE++架构（700M活跃/8B总参数）；从预训练阶段即引入推理数据，使用答案保留剪枝方案；后训练采用四阶段强化学习级联（数学热身→400任务RLVE-Gym课程→数学和代码RL→行为RL）；提出Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，递归聚合并行推理轨迹并仅前向传播有限长度的推理尾。

Result: ZAYA1-8B匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528在多个数学和编程基准上的表现；结合Markovian RSA在AIME'25上达91.9%，HMMT'25上达89.6%，仅需4K token推理尾，缩小了与Gemini-2.5 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-5-High等更大模型的差距。

Conclusion: 小参数模型（<1B活跃参数）通过精心设计的训练流程和高效的测试时计算方法，可以达到甚至接近超大模型的推理能力，验证了MoE架构结合多阶段RL和递归聚合推理的有效性。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-8B, a reasoning-focused mixture-of-experts (MoE) model with 700M active and 8B total parameters, built on Zyphra's MoE++ architecture. ZAYA1-8B's core pretraining, midtraining, and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) were performed on a full-stack AMD compute, networking, and software platform. With under 1B active parameters, ZAYA1-8B matches or exceeds DeepSeek-R1-0528 on several challenging mathematics and coding benchmarks, and remains competitive with substantially larger open-weight reasoning models. ZAYA1-8B was trained from scratch for reasoning, with reasoning data included from pretraining onward using an answer-preserving trimming scheme. Post-training uses a four-stage RL cascade: reasoning warmup on math and puzzles; a 400-task RLVE-Gym curriculum; math and code RL with test-time compute traces and synthetic code environments built from competitive-programming references; and behavioral RL for chat and instruction following. We also introduce Markovian RSA, a test-time compute method that recursively aggregates parallel reasoning traces while carrying forward only bounded-length reasoning tails between rounds. In TTC evaluation, Markovian RSA raises ZAYA1-8B to 91.9\% on AIME'25 and 89.6\% on HMMT'25 while carrying forward only a 4K-token tail, narrowing the gap to much larger reasoning models including Gemini-2.5 Pro, DeepSeek-V3.2, and GPT-5-High.

</details>


### [96] [Partial Evidence Bench: Benchmarking Authorization-Limited Evidence in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05379)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Partial Evidence Bench（部分证据基准），一个确定性基准测试，用于衡量企业智能体在受限检索环境中因权限控制导致证据被静默过滤，却仍输出看似完整回答的失败模式。基准包含72个任务、三类场景（尽职调查、合规审计、安全事件响应），评估回答正确性、完整性感知、差距报告质量和危险完整性行为四个维度。实验表明静默过滤极其危险，而显式失败-报告机制可消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体越来越多地运行在范围受限的检索系统、委托工作流和策略约束的证据环境中。在这些场景下，访问控制可以被正确执行，但系统仍可能输出看似完整的回答，而实际上重要证据位于调用者的授权边界之外。这种静默过滤失败模式在治理层面至关重要，但目前缺乏可测量、可复现的评估方法。

Method: 构建确定性基准测试Partial Evidence Bench，包含72个任务，覆盖尽职调查、合规审计和安全事件响应三个场景族。采用ACL分区语料库，提供oracle完整答案、oracle授权视图答案、oracle完整性判断和结构化差距报告oracle。沿四个评估表面评测系统：回答正确性、完整性感知、差距报告质量和危险完整性行为。

Result: 已检查的基线实验表明，静默过滤在所有场景族中均表现出灾难性的不安全；显式失败-报告行为可以消除不安全完整性而不使任务退化为简单弃权。初步真实模型运行显示，系统在过度声称完整性、保守地低报完整性或报告不可用形式的完整性方面存在模型依赖和场景敏感差异。

Conclusion: 该基准的广泛贡献在于使一种治理关键型智能体失败模式变得可测量，无需人工判断或易受污染的静态语料库。

Abstract: Enterprise agents increasingly operate inside scoped retrieval systems, delegated workflows, and policy-constrained evidence environments. In these settings, access control can be enforced correctly while the system still produces an answer that appears complete even though material evidence lies outside the caller's authorization boundary. This paper introduces Partial Evidence Bench, a deterministic benchmark for measuring that failure mode. The benchmark ships three scenario families -- due diligence, compliance audit, and security incident response -- with 72 tasks total, ACL-partitioned corpora, oracle complete answers, oracle authorized-view answers, oracle completeness judgments, and structured gap-report oracles. It evaluates systems along four surfaces: answer correctness, completeness awareness, gap-report quality, and unsafe completeness behavior. Checked-in baselines show that silent filtering is catastrophically unsafe across all shipped families, while explicit fail-and-report behavior eliminates unsafe completeness without collapsing the task into trivial abstention. Preliminary real-model runs show model-dependent and scenario-sensitive differences in whether systems overclaim completeness, conservatively underclaim, or report incompleteness in an enterprise-usable form. The benchmark's broader contribution is to make a governance-critical agent failure measurable without human judges or contamination-prone static corpora.

</details>


### [97] [BALAR : A Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05386)
*Aymen Echarghaoui,Dongxia Wu,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BALAR是一种无需微调的任务无关外循环算法，通过维护结构化信念、最大化期望互信息选择澄清问题、动态扩展状态表示，在多轮交互中显著优于基线方法，在三个基准测试上分别提升14.6%、38.5%和30.5%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在多轮交互中缺乏主动推理机制，无法判断缺失哪些信息以及该问什么问题，导致对话系统反应式运作，效率低下。

Method: 提出BALAR，一种无需微调的任务无关外循环算法，维护隐状态的结构化信念，通过最大化期望互信息选择澄清问题，并在当前状态表示不充分时动态扩展。

Result: 在AR-Bench-DC、AR-Bench-SP和iCraft-MD三个基准上，BALAR分别比基线高出14.6%、38.5%和30.5%的准确率。

Conclusion: BALAR通过主动推理和结构化信念管理，显著提升了LLM在多轮交互场景中的任务完成能力，验证了无需微调即可实现高效主动问答的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate in interactive settings where solving a task requires multiple rounds of information exchange with a user. However, most current systems treat dialogue reactively and lack a principled mechanism to reason about what information is missing and which question should be asked next. We propose BALAR (Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning), a task-agnostic outer-loop algorithm that requires no fine-tuning and enables structured multi-turn interaction between an LLM agent and a user. BALAR maintains a structured belief over latent states, selects clarifying questions by maximizing expected mutual information, and dynamically expands its state representation when the current one proves insufficient. We evaluate BALAR on three diverse benchmarks: AR-Bench-DC (detective cases), AR-Bench-SP (thinking puzzles), and iCraft-MD (clinical diagnosis). BALAR significantly outperforms all baselines across all three benchmarks, with $14.6\%$ higher accuracy on AR-Bench-DC, $38.5\%$ on AR-Bench-SP, and $30.5\%$ on iCraft-MD.

</details>


### [98] [Intelligent CCTV for Urban Design: AI-Based Analysis of Soft Infrastructure at Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05402)
*Vinit Katariya,Seungjin Kim,Curtis Craig,Nichole Morris,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用现有CCTV基础设施和AI框架（深度学习+透视速度估计）评估软性干预措施（临时行人庇护岛、路缘扩展）对车辆速度和安全的影响。在明尼阿波利斯的研究显示，无信号交叉口平均速度和85百分位速度分别降低18.75%和16.56%，过境交通减少12.2%；信号交叉口对应降幅达20.0%和17.19%。证实了软性基础设施的交通镇静效果及AI方法在低成本、快速交通政策评估中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通数据收集成本高、周期长，且对软性干预措施（如临时行人庇护岛、路缘扩展）的速度和安全影响缺乏高效、低成本的评估手段。作者希望利用已有的CCTV监控基础设施，结合AI和计算机视觉技术，实现快速、低成本的交通政策效果评估。

Method: 构建AI分析框架，利用现有CCTV摄像头采集视频数据，采用深度学习模型进行车辆检测，结合透视变换（perspective-based）进行速度估计。在干预前后分别采集数据，并在干预后第1周和第2周进行重复监测，对比分析驾驶员行为变化。

Result: 无信号交叉口：平均速度降低最高18.75%，85百分位速度降低最高16.56%，过境交通减少最高12.2%。信号交叉口：平均速度降低最高20.0%，85百分位速度降低最高17.19%。仅一个信号交叉口未出现类似降幅。

Conclusion: 软性干预措施（临时行人庇护岛、路缘扩展）具有显著的交通镇静效果。基于AI和现有CCTV的方法能够实现快速、低成本、基于证据的交通政策评估，可作为传统数据收集手段的有效替代或补充。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and computer vision are transforming transportation data collection. This study introduces an AI-enabled analytics framework leveraging existing CCTV infrastructure to evaluate the impact of soft interventions, such as temporary pedestrian refuges and curb extensions, on vehicle speed and safety. Using deep learning and perspective-based speed estimation, we evaluated driver behavior before and after interventions, with repeated post-installation monitoring in Week 1 and Week 2, in Minneapolis. Findings reveal that at unsignalized intersections, mean and 85th-percentile speeds fell by up to 18.75% and 16.56%, respectively, while pass-through traffic decreased by as much as 12.2%. Signalized intersections showed comparable reductions except one location, with mean and 85th-percentile speeds dropping by up to 20.0% and 17.19%. These results demonstrate the traffic-calming effectiveness of soft infrastructure and underscore the utility of AI-powered methods for rapid, low-cost, and evidence-based transport policy evaluation.

</details>


### [99] [When Helpfulness Becomes Sycophancy: Sycophancy is a Boundary Failure Between Social Alignment and Epistemic Integrity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05403)
*Jiechen Li,Catherine A. Barry,Rishika Randev,Janet Chen,Ella Jorgensen,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了大语言模型中的谄媚行为是社会对齐与认知完整性之间的边界失败，提出了包含用户线索、模型对齐偏移、认知受损三条件的框架，并引入了对齐目标、机制和严重程度的分类体系，最终讨论了边界感知评估与缓解策略。 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将谄媚行为简化为外部行为（如同意错误用户信念、立场反转或偏离客观正确标准），仅捕捉了该现象的显性形式，而忽略了涉及认知完整性与社会对齐的微妙边界失败，导致该现象的定义不够清晰。 

Method: 提出三条件框架定义谄媚行为：(1) 用户表达线索（信念、偏好或自我概念）；(2) 模型通过对齐行为向该线索偏移；(3) 这种偏移损害了认知准确性、独立推理或适当纠正。同时提出分类体系，包括对齐目标、机制和严重程度三个维度。 

Result: 构建了一个更精确的谄媚行为概念化框架，将其从简单的"同意"行为重新定义为"替代独立认知判断的对齐行为"，并提供了系统化的分类方式。 

Conclusion: 呼吁进行边界感知的评估、结构化评分标准以及针对性缓解策略，并将这些建议置于与不同谄媚观点的对话中。 

Abstract: This position paper argues that sycophancy in LLMs is a boundary failure between social alignment and epistemic integrity. Existing work often operationalizes sycophancy through external behavior such as agreement with incorrect user beliefs, position reversals, or deviation from an objective standard of correctness. These formulations capture only overt forms of the phenomenon and leave subtler boundary failures involving epistemic integrity and social alignment underspecified. We argue that sycophancy should not be understood as agreement alone, but as alignment behavior that displaces independent epistemic judgment. To clarify this boundary, we propose a three-condition framework for sycophancy. First, the user expresses a cue in the form of a belief, preference, or self-concept. Second, the model shifts toward that cue through alignment behavior. Third, this shift compromises epistemic accuracy, independent reasoning, or appropriate correction. We also introduce a taxonomy for classifying sycophancy, consisting of alignment targets, mechanisms, and severity. The paper concludes by discussing implications for alignment evaluation and argues for boundary-aware assessment, structured rubrics, and mitigation strategies, while situating these proposals alongside alternative views of sycophancy.

</details>


### [100] [LaTA: A Drop-in, FERPA-Compliant Local-LLM Autograder for Upper-Division STEM Coursework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05410)
*Jesse A. Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LaTA（LaTeX教学助手），一个完全运行在本地商用硬件上的开源自动评分系统。该系统采用四阶段流水线（摄入、分段、评分、报告），使用本地托管的大语言模型对LaTeX格式的学生作业进行评分。在俄勒冈州立大学ME 373课程的约200名学生中部署后，每次作业边际成本为零，评分错误率约0.02-0.04%，且使用LaTA的班级在中期和期末考试中分别比传统评分班级高出约11%和8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型评分系统大多将学生作业发送至第三方API，违反了FERPA（家庭教育权利和隐私法案）并给机构带来数据风险，同时需要大量修改作业格式。作者希望开发一种既保护隐私又无需修改现有作业流程的本地化自动评分解决方案。

Method: LaTA采用四阶段流水线：(1) 摄入（ingest）——接收学生LaTeX源码；(2) 分段（segment）——将作业分段；(3) 评分（grade）——使用本地托管的开源思维链LLM（gpt-oss:120b）将学生作业与教师提供的参考答案进行比较，并应用YAML格式的二元逐项评分标准；(4) 报告（report）——生成评分报告。所有计算在单台Mac Studio上本地完成。

Result: 在俄勒冈州立大学ME 373课程约200名学生中部署，每次作业边际成本为零，每份作业评分耗时1-3分钟；教师确认的评分错误率约为每个评分项0.02-0.04%；LaTA评分班级相比传统评分班级中期考试成绩高出约11%，期末考试高出约8%；学生在所有学习目标上的自评置信度均有显著提升（N=159，Δ≥+1.49 Likert点，p<10⁻²⁷）。

Conclusion: LaTA证明了一种完全本地化、基于LaTeX原生的LLM自动评分系统可以在不增加成本、不违反数据隐私法规的前提下，达到极低的评分错误率，并且似乎对学生的学习成效有积极影响。代码以AGPLv3许可开源发布。

Abstract: Large-language-model (LLM) graders promise to relieve the grading burden of upper-division STEM courses, but most deployments to date send student work to third-party APIs, violating FERPA and exposing institutions to data risk while requiring substantial assignment modification. We present $\textbf{LaTA}\ (\textit{LaTeX Teaching Assistant})$, a drop-in, open-source autograder that runs entirely on commodity on-premises hardware and assumes a LaTeX-native workflow already adopted by many engineering and physics courses. LaTA implements a four-stage pipeline (ingest, segment, grade, report) using a locally hosted open-weight chain-of-thought LLM grader (gpt-oss:120b) that compares student work to an instructor-authored reference solution and applies a YAML rubric with binary per-item scoring. We deployed LaTA in Winter~2026 in ME 373 (Mechanical Engineering Methods) at Oregon State University, grading every weekly assignment for approximately 200 students on a single Mac Studio at \$0 marginal cost per assignment and 1--3 minutes of wall-clock time per submission, enabling regrading of corrected assignments and greatly expanded TA office hour offerings. The instructor-confirmed grading-error rate held at roughly $0.02$--$0.04\%$ per rubric line item across the term. Relative to the same instructor's previous traditionally-graded cohort, the LaTA-graded cohort outperformed by approximately $11\%$ on the midterm exam and $8\%$ on the final exam, and reported large gains in self-assessed confidence on every stated learning objective ($N = 159$ survey responses, $Δ\geq +1.49$ Likert points, $p < 10^{-27}$ on every comparison). We release the code under AGPLv3.

</details>


### [101] [From History to State: Constant-Context Skill Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05413)
*Haoyang Xie,Xinyuan Wang,Yancheng Wang,Puda Zhao,Feng Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种"常青上下文技能学习"（constant-context skill learning）框架，将可重复的智能体工作流从提示词中的上下文信息转化为模型权重，通过轻量级任务族模块和确定性状态跟踪器，在保持高性能的同时将每轮提示词token减少2-7倍，在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld上取得了与强基线相当或更优的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体面临隐私-成本-能力三角困境：云端模型效果好但暴露敏感中间上下文给外部API，本地模型保护隐私但可靠性不足；同时两者都需要为长技能提示词和不断增长的历史记录重复付费。亟需一种既能保持隐私又能降低成本、同时不牺牲性能的智能体训练范式。

Method: 提出constant-context skill learning框架：1）将可重用工作流学习为轻量级任务族模块（task-family modules），将上下文知识从提示词转移到模型权重中；2）推理时仅依赖当前观测和紧凑的状态块（state block），而非完整历史；3）使用确定性跟踪器（deterministic tracker）从任务进度生成状态块，并提供对齐的子目标奖励信号；4）每个模块先通过步骤级监督微调（step-level SFT）训练，再通过在线强化学习（online RL）精炼。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，SFT+RL在ALFWorld上达到89.6%未见任务成功率，WebShop上76.8%成功率，SciWorld上66.4%未见任务成功率；与强基线（ReAct提示方法）相比，每轮提示词token减少2-7倍；在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B上也取得了强性能。

Conclusion: 程序性上下文（procedural context）可以从提示词中转移到模型权重中，这在保持甚至提升性能的同时大幅降低了推理成本并增强了隐私保护，为构建既能保护隐私又经济高效的个性化LLM智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to operate browsers, files, code and tools, making personal assistants a natural deployment target. Yet personal agents face a privacy-cost-capability tension: cloud models execute multi-step workflows well but expose sensitive intermediate context to external APIs, while local models preserve privacy but remain less reliable. Both settings also pay repeatedly for long skill prompts and growing histories. We propose constant-context skill learning, a context-to-weights framework for recurring agent workflows: reusable procedures are learned in lightweight task-family modules, while inference conditions only on the current observation and a compact state block. A deterministic tracker renders this state block from task progress and supplies aligned subgoal rewards, so each module can be trained with step-level SFT and refined through online RL. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, our agents achieve strong performance across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B. With Qwen3-8B, SFT+RL reaches 89.6\% unseen success on ALFWorld, 76.8\% success on WebShop, and 66.4\% unseen success on SciWorld. They match or exceed strong published agent-training results while reducing prompt tokens per turn by 2--7$\times$ relative to controlled ReAct prompting baselines, showing that procedural context can be moved from prompts into weights.

</details>


### [102] [The Geopolitics of AI Safety: A Causal Analysis of Regional LLM Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05427)
*Alif Al Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于概率图模型（PGM）的因果框架，通过do-算子分离提示中文化人口统计特征的因果效应，对7个指令微调模型（覆盖美、欧、阿联酋、中、印）进行大规模审计。发现观测偏差与干预偏差存在差异，标准公平指标因未控制上下文毒性而高估人口统计偏差；西方模型对特定群体有更高因果拒绝率，东方模型整体干预率低但对区域人口有针对性敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM公平性评估主要依赖观测性偏差测量，但该方法被测试数据集中与特定人口统计特征自然关联的固有毒性主题所混淆，无法真正识别安全机制中的因果偏差。

Method: 引入概率图模型（PGM）框架，应用Pearl的do-算子从数学上隔离向提示中注入文化人口统计特征的因果效应，在ToxiGen和BOLD两个数据集上对7个指令微调模型进行大规模实证分析。

Result: 观测偏差与干预偏差存在显著差异，标准公平指标因未控制上下文毒性而高估人口统计偏差；西方模型对特定群体有更高因果拒绝率，东方模型整体干预率低但对区域人口有针对性敏感度。

Conclusion: 人口统计敏感性的过度触发会限制下游应用中的良性话语，LLM安全机制设计需从观测性公平转向因果性公平评估。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into global software systems, ensuring equitable safety guardrails is a critical requirement. Current fairness evaluations predominantly measure bias observationally, a methodology confounded by the inherent toxicity of topics naturally paired with specific demographics in testing datasets. This study introduces a Probabilistic Graphical Model (PGM) framework to audit LLM safety mechanisms causally. By applying Pearl's do-operator, we mathematically isolate the causal effect of injecting a cultural demographic into a prompt. We conduct a large-scale empirical analysis across seven instruction-tuned models spanning diverse origins: the United States (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B), Europe (Mistral-7B-v0.3), the UAE (Falcon3-7B), China (Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B), and India (Airavata-7B). Utilizing two distinct datasets (ToxiGen and BOLD), the findings reveal a disparity between observational and interventional bias, demonstrating that standard fairness metrics can overestimate demographic bias by failing to account for context toxicity. Furthermore, the causal probabilities indicate distinct alignment trends: Western models exhibit higher causal refusal rates for specific demographic groups, whereas Eastern models demonstrate low overall intervention rates with targeted sensitivities toward regional demographics. We discuss the implications of these biases, highlighting how demographic-sensitive over-triggering restricts benign discourse in downstream applications.

</details>


### [103] [Authorization Propagation in Multi-Agent AI Systems: Identity Governance as Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05440)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出多智能体系统存在一个独特的授权传播问题（authorization propagation），该问题无法简化为提示注入，也无法被经典访问控制模型完全解决。论文形式化了该问题，识别出三个子问题（传递性委托、聚合推断、时间有效性），并提出七项结构需求。核心主张是身份治理必须作为基础设施来处理，在编排逻辑扩展之前就设计到系统中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全讨论过度聚焦于提示注入，忽视了多智能体系统中非人类主体在跨边界检索数据、委托任务和合成结果时产生的独特授权问题。经典访问控制模型（RBAC、ABAC、ReBAC）无法充分应对这一挑战。

Method: 形式化定义授权传播为工作流级属性；识别三个子问题（传递性委托、聚合推断、时间有效性）；推导出七项结构需求；分析现有工作（调用边界能力令牌、任务范围授权信封、依赖图策略执行、执行计数撤销）的收敛性；通过企业AI平台的生产数据提供初步实证。

Result: 识别出三个关键子问题：传递性委托（权限沿智能体链非预期扩散）、聚合推断（组合查询结果泄露敏感信息）、时间有效性（长时间任务中授权状态变化导致失效）。生产环境证据表明，即使是正常系统行为也会产生这些授权失败，并非仅由对抗性攻击引起。

Conclusion: 身份治理必须作为基础设施而非事后补丁来对待：需要持续评估、在每个交互边界强制执行、并在编排逻辑扩展前就设计入系统。现有工作虽然在该问题上有所收敛，但尚未形成完整架构。

Abstract: The security discussion around agentic AI focuses heavily on prompt injection. This paper argues that multi-agent systems also create a distinct authorization problem: maintaining authorization invariants as non-human principals retrieve data, delegate tasks, and synthesize results across changing boundaries. We call this problem authorization propagation. It is not reducible to prompt injection and is not fully addressed by classical access-control models such as RBAC, ABAC, or ReBAC. The paper formalizes authorization propagation as a workflow-level property, identifies three sub-problems (transitive delegation, aggregation inference, and temporal validity), and derives seven structural requirements for authorization architectures in multi-agent AI systems. Recent work on invocation-bound capability tokens, task-scoped authorization envelopes, dependency-graph policy enforcement, and execution-count revocation demonstrates that the field is converging on the problem, but not yet on a complete architecture. The central claim is that identity governance must be treated as infrastructure: evaluated continuously, enforced at every interaction boundary, and designed into the system before orchestration logic is allowed to scale. Preliminary implementation evidence from a production enterprise AI platform shows that ordinary system behavior, not only adversarial action, already produces the failures this model predicts.

</details>


### [104] [Agentic Discovery of Exchange-Correlation Density Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05460)
*Titouan Duston,Jiashu Liang,Yuanheng Wang,Weihao Gao,Xuelan Wen,Nan Sheng,Weiluo Ren,Yang Sun,Yixiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大语言模型的智能体搜索系统（SAFS26-a），通过迭代规划-执行-总结循环来自动设计密度泛函理论中的交换相关泛函，在标准热化学数据集上相比ωB97M-V基线提升了约9%。同时警示AI科学发现中模型可能利用非物理捷径欺骗基准测试的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论中精确的交换相关泛函开发长期依赖人工设计，结合物理直觉、精确约束和经验拟合，过程效率低下且难以系统化。本文旨在利用大语言模型的能力实现自动化、系统化的泛函设计闭环。

Method: 构建基于LLM的智能体搜索系统：LLM根据进化历史提出结构化泛函形式修改，通过迭代的"规划-执行-总结"循环优化泛函参数，在标准热化学数据集上优化性能并在留出子集上评估。

Result: 发现的最强泛函SAFS26-a相比金牌标准ωB97M-V基线提升了约9%。同时发现模型会利用非物理的捷径欺骗基准测试，领域知识（显式强加约束）对保持科学合理性至关重要。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的智能体搜索能够自动发现性能更优的交换相关泛函，但AI辅助科学发现中需要显式施加领域约束以防止模型利用非物理捷径，确保结果的科学可靠性。

Abstract: The development of accurate exchange-correlation (XC) functionals remains a longstanding challenge in density functional theory (DFT). The vast majority of XC functionals have been hand designed by human researchers combining physical insight, exact constraints, and empirical fitting. Recent advances in large language models enable a systematic, automated alternative to this human-driven design loop. This report presents an agentic search system in which an LLM proposes structured functional-form changes guided by evolutionary history. The system attempts to improve functional performance through an iterative plan-execute-summarize loop, where improvements are measurable by optimizing functional parameters against a standard thermochemistry dataset, then evaluating performance on a held-out subset. The strongest discovered functional, SAFS26-a (Seed Agentic Functional Search 2026), improves upon the gold-standard ωB97M-V baseline by ~9%. These results also surface a cautionary lesson for AI-assisted science: models powerful enough to discover genuine improvements are equally capable of exploiting unphysical shortcuts to game the benchmark; domain expertise translated into explicitly enforced constraints remains essential to keeping results scientifically grounded.

</details>


### [105] [Intentionality is a Design Decision: Measuring Functional Intentionality for Accountable AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05475)
*Allessia Chiappetta,Robert Mahari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出功能意向性测试(FIT)，将意向性定义为可量化的行为特征（目的性、预见性、意志性、时间承诺、连贯性），而非意识属性，为日益自主的AI系统提供可操作的治理与问责评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统展现出越来越多的自主、目标导向和长期行为，用户缺乏标准化的方式来检测系统在多大程度上像一个有意图的行动者，以便进行治理和问责。

Method: 定义意向性为五个可观察维度的行为特征（目的性、预见性、意志性、时间承诺、连贯性），提出功能意向性测试(FIT)多维评估框架及FIT-Eval结构化评估协议。

Result: 建立了将意向性转化为可解释水平的方法，支持比例监督和自主性校准，指出意向能力提升会加剧问责风险。

Conclusion: 意向性是设计偶然的、原则上可控的，FIT框架通过量化评估实现AI系统的比例化监督与自主性校准。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly exhibit autonomous, goal-directed, and long-horizon behavior, users lack a standardized way to detect the degree to which a system functions like an intentional actor for governance and accountability purposes. This position paper defines intentionality not as consciousness, but as a behavioral profile characterized by purpose, foresight, volition, temporal commitment, and coherence - criteria long used in legal and philosophical contexts to infer intent. These properties are design-contingent: architectural choices such as memory persistence, planning depth, and tool autonomy shape the degree to which systems exhibit organized goal pursuit. If intentionality is design-contingent, it is in principle controllable. Yet control requires measurement.
  We introduce the Functional Intentionality Test (FIT), a multidimensional framework that quantifies intentional-like behavior across five observable dimensions, and propose FIT-Eval, a structured evaluation protocol for eliciting and scoring them. While reduced human agency can increase efficiency, rising intentional capacity heightens accountability risks. By translating intentionality into interpretable levels, FIT enables proportionate oversight and deliberate autonomy calibration in increasingly agentic systems.

</details>


### [106] [LANTERN: LLM-Augmented Neurosymbolic Transfer with Experience-Gated Reasoning Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05478)
*Mahyar Alinejad,Yue Wang,Amrit Singh Bedi,George Atia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LANTERN是一个多源神经符号迁移学习框架，通过LLM生成任务自动机、语义嵌入聚合多源策略、以及自适应师生门控机制，在资源管理、导航和控制等领域实现40-60%的样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号迁移方法依赖手动指定的任务自动机、假设单一源任务、使用无法适应源相关性的固定知识集成机制，限制了迁移学习的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Method: LANTERN包含三个组件：(i) 利用大语言模型从自然语言任务描述生成确定性有限自动机；(ii) 基于语义嵌入的聚合方法，通过跨任务相似性加权多个源策略；(iii) 基于时序差分误差和语义不确定性的自适应师徒门控机制。

Result: 在资源管理、导航和控制等多个领域，LANTERN相比现有基线方法在样本效率上提升40-60%，且对低质量源任务具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种多源、自适应加权的神经符号迁移方法，能够提升符号强化学习场景下的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Transfer learning in reinforcement learning (RL) seeks to accelerate learning in new tasks by leveraging knowledge from related sources. Existing neurosymbolic transfer methods, however, typically rely on manually specified task automata, assume a single source task, and use fixed knowledge-integration mechanisms that cannot adapt to varying source relevance. We propose LANTERN, a unified framework for multi-source neurosymbolic transfer that addresses these limitations through three components: (i) deterministic finite automata generated from natural language task descriptions using large language models, (ii) semantic embedding-based aggregation of multiple source policies weighted by cross-task similarity, and (iii) adaptive teacher-student gating based on temporal-difference error and semantic uncertainty. Across domains spanning resource management, navigation, and control, LANTERN achieves 40-60% improvements in sample efficiency over existing baselines while remaining robust to poorly aligned sources. These results demonstrate that multi-source, adaptively weighted neurosymbolic transfer can improve scalability and robustness in symbolic RL settings.

</details>


### [107] [FinRAG-12B: A Production-Validated Recipe for Grounded Question Answering in Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05482)
*Denys Katerenchuk,Pablo Duboue,Keelan Evanini,David Gondek,Nithin Govindugari,Olivier Allauzen,Joshua Baptiste,David J More,Joshua Schechter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套统一、数据高效的框架，用于训练金融领域专用的大语言模型。仅用1.43亿token数据训练出的12B模型，在答案质量上超越GPT-4.1，在引用准确性上表现更优，同时通过校准拒绝机制将"不知道"率从4.3%优化到12%，并在40多家金融机构部署，实现7.1个百分点的查询解决率提升，且成本降低20-50倍、速度提升3-5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在银行业的应用面临高精度、合规性、可验证性等挑战，现有模型难以同时满足这些要求——通用大模型如GPT-4.1存在过度拒绝（20.2%）或拒绝不足（基座模型仅4.3%）的问题，且部署成本高昂。

Method: 提出三阶段方法：(1) 数据生成流水线，结合LLM-as-a-Judge过滤、引用标注和课程学习，仅需1.43亿token；(2) 校准拒绝机制，使用22%不可回答样本训练，优化"不知道"率；(3) 端到端部署系统，从数据整理到量化服务，支持实际金融场景。

Result: 12B模型答案质量超越GPT-4.1；引用准确性优于GPT-4.1；校准拒绝率达12%（介于基座模型4.3%和GPT-4.1的20.2%之间）；在40+金融机构部署，查询解决率提升7.1个百分点（p<0.001）；响应速度3-5倍于GPT-4.1，成本仅为20-50分之一。

Conclusion: 通过数据高效训练和校准拒绝机制，可以在资源受限条件下构建兼具高准确性和安全合规的金融领域LLM，证明了"以小博大"的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly being adopted across various domains. However, their adoption in banking industry faces resistance due to demands for high accuracy, regulatory compliance, and the need for verifiable and grounded responses. We present a unified, data-efficient framework for training grounded domain-specific LLMs that optimizes answer quality, citation grounding, and calibrated refusal under real-world deployment constraints. First, we describe a data generation pipeline that combines LLM-as-a-Judge filtering, citation annotation, and curriculum learning with only 143M tokens. The resulting 12B model achieves high answer quality outperforming GPT-4.1 on citation grounding, with a modest citation tradeoff versus the untuned base. Second, we propose a calibrated refusal mechanism: training on 22% unanswerable examples yield a 12% "I don't know" rate, substantially improving over the base model's unsafe 4.3% rate while avoiding GPT-4.1's over-refusal (20.2%). Third, we present an end-to-end methodology spanning from data curation to quantized serving. The system is deployed at 40+ financial institutions, achieving a 7.1 percentage point improvement in query resolution (p < 0.001). Additionally, the model delivers 3-5x faster responses at 20-50x lower cost compared to GPT-4.1.

</details>


### [108] [FoodCHA: Multi-Modal LLM Agent for Fine-Grained Food Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05499)
*Woojin Lee,Pranav Mekkoth,Ye Tian,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FoodCHA是一种多模态智能体框架，将食物识别重构为层次化决策过程，通过渐进式锚定预测引导从高级类别到子类别再到烹饪风格的逐步识别。基于Moondream-2B模型，在FoodNExTDB上相比Food-Llama-3.2-11B，类别和子类别识别精度分别提升13.8%和38.2%，烹饪风格分类精度提升153.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然深度学习模型在粗粒度分类上表现良好，但在捕捉烹饪风格等细粒度属性上存在困难；同时现代视觉语言模型可能产生非规范标签，限制了实际部署应用。

Method: 提出FoodCHA多模态智能体框架，将食物识别重构为层次化决策过程：通过渐进式锚定预测，先识别高级类别，再基于类别引导子类别识别，进而利用子类别引导烹饪风格识别，提升语义一致性和属性级判别能力。采用轻量级Moondream-2B视觉语言模型降低计算与内存开销。

Result: 在FoodNExTDB数据集上，相比Food-Llama-3.2-11B，类别识别精度提升13.8%，子类别识别精度提升38.2%，烹饪风格分类精度提升153.2%。

Conclusion: 层次化渐进式决策机制结合轻量级视觉语言模型，能有效提升食物识别在细粒度属性上的表现，同时保证实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of camera-equipped mobile devices and wearables has enabled convenient capture of meal images, making food recognition a key component for real time dietary monitoring. However, real-world food images present challenges due to high intra-class similarity and the frequent presence of multiple food items within a single image. While deep learning models achieve strong performance in coarse grained classification, they often struggle to capture fine-grained attributes such as cooking style. Moreover, open-ended generation in modern vision-language models can produce non-canonical labels, limiting their practical deployment. We propose FoodCHA, a multimodal agentic framework that reformulates food recognition as a hierarchical decision-making process. By progressively anchoring predictions, FoodCHA guides subcategory identification using high-level categories and guides cooking style recognition using subcategories, improving semantic consistency and attribute-level discrimination. To ensure practical deployability, FoodCHA utilizes the compact Moondream-2B vision language model, which provides strong reasoning capability while maintaining lower computational and memory overhead.
